[1] MeddeX: A Retrieval-Augmented LLaMA-3 System with Gemini Vision for Evidence-Backed Medical Report Analysis cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Rajdeep Pandhare, Rohit Kundaikar, Yakub Kojar, Shreyansh Tiwari

TL;DR: MeddeX结合QLoRA微调LLaMA-3、检索增强生成和Gemini视觉层，分析文本PDF、扫描PDF及放射图像，并通过报告级检索和纵向比较生成带证据摘要。

Details

Motivation: 医疗报告日益复杂，人工审阅耗时、易错且难以扩展；系统需处理文本原生PDF、扫描图像PDF和影像，并为问答提供报告上下文依据。

Result: MeddeX以8B LLaMA-3经QLoRA微调，结合RAG和多模态Gemini视觉层。混合提取流程包含针对损坏及UTF-16编码文档的垃圾文本检测；系统还提供报告范围检索、自动纵向报告比较，以及按需回填旧文档的自修复流程。相较LLaMA3-Med42-8B基座，评测ROUGE-1提高9.2%、ROUGE-2提高10.0%、ROUGE-L提高11.8%、BLEU提高71.4%、BERTScore提高0.8%。系统由FastAPI、Supabase向量库和Next.js前端组成。

Insight: 报告级检索和纵向比较可把摘要与特定文档证据及时间变化关联；多格式提取则覆盖文本与扫描输入。摘要中的自动文本指标提升不能等同于临床正确性或决策支持有效性，未报告医生评审、错误率或前瞻临床验证。

Abstract: Modern healthcare generates increasingly complex medical reports that challenge clinical professionals in terms of time and interpretive burden. Manual review processes are slow, error-prone, and difficult to scale. This paper presents MeddeX, a Medical Report Analyzer that combines a QLoRA fine-tuned LLaMA-3 (8B) model with Retrieval-Augmented Gen eration (RAG) and a multimodal Gemini Vision layer to deliver accurate, concise, and evidence-backed summaries of medical documents. The system handles text-native PDFs, scanned image based PDFs, and direct radiological images through an intelligent hybrid extraction pipeline that includes a novel garbage-text detection mechanism for corrupted and UTF-16-encoded doc uments. Beyond single-report Q&A, MeddeX introduces report scoped retrieval for precise contextual grounding, an automated longitudinal report comparison engine, and a self-healing backfill pipeline that lazily processes legacy documents on demand. A FastAPI backend, Supabase vector store, and Next.js fron tend complete the end-to-end architecture. Evaluation against the base LLaMA3-Med42-8B model demonstrates consistent improvements across ROUGE-1 (+9.2%), ROUGE-2 (+10.0%), ROUGE-L (+11.8%), BLEU (+71.4%), and BERTScore (+0.8%), validating the benefit of domain-specific fine-tuning. MeddeX represents a practical and robust step toward AI-assisted clinical decision support.

[2] AbridgeKV: Compressing Long Reasoning into the KV Cache of Learned Pause Tokens cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Jack Cai, Lijie Yang, Vasilis Kontonis, Dimitris Papailiopoulos, Shivam Garg

TL;DR: AbridgeKV将已生成的长推理文本压缩到少量可学习暂停token的KV缓存中，在保留准确率和输出长度的同时显著降低缓存占用并加速推理。

Details

Motivation: 长链式推理的KV缓存会随生成token线性增长，造成推理的内存瓶颈；直接淘汰缓存条目会让未在这种缓存状态上训练过的模型生成更长文本，无法有效解决问题。

Result: AbridgeKV在每个生成文本块后插入若干带训练嵌入、但不产生输出的暂停token，让其KV表示压缩该块，再释放原始缓存。方法将暂停token置于被替换文本块的内部位置，按层中缓存条目的测量价值分配预算，并使用较大的压缩块。在AIME、HMMT、AMC、BRUMO、CMIMC和SMT共446道竞赛题、32K token生成设置中，保留四分之一生成缓存时，准确率与输出长度匹配同数据训练的全KV模型；同预算下优于最强已发表淘汰方法，且比最接近的同数据重训压缩器高10个百分点。vLLM部署时每序列缓存缩小3.5倍，饱和H100完成一道题的速度为全KV的3.2倍。

Insight: 结果表明，训练模型主动压缩自身历史可能比推理时直接删缓存更有效，尤其适合长推理的显存和吞吐优化。摘要报告的是竞赛数学任务和特定服务设置，不能直接推断其他任务、模型或硬件上的收益。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought decoding is memory-bound: the KV cache of the generated tokens grows linearly with the test-time compute being spent. Evicting cache entries does not fix this, because a model never trained on a cache with entries removed generates longer, often up to the length limit. We instead train the model to compress its own output. AbridgeKV compresses each completed block of generated text into the KV entries of a few learned pause tokens. These are extra input tokens with trained embeddings that produce no output. Once the pause tokens have encoded the block, AbridgeKV frees the block’s original cache; subsequent tokens attend to the pause tokens’ KV entries in its place. Three novel choices make this work: the pause tokens’ KV entries take positions inside the block they replace, the cache budget is allocated across layers according to the measured value of each layer’s entries, and compression operates on relatively large blocks. On 446 competition problems from AIME, HMMT, AMC, BRUMO, CMIMC and SMT with 32K-token generations, keeping a quarter of the generated cache matches an identically trained full-KV model in accuracy and output length; the strongest published evictor at a matched budget writes far longer, and the closest trained compressor, retrained on the same data, loses 10 points. Served in vLLM, the cache is 3.5x smaller per sequence and a saturated H100 finishes a problem 3.2x faster than with full KV.

[3] CoTS: Competitive Token Selection for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jie Yu, Yuxi Sun, Zhaoxu Li, Chunxiao Liu, Xudong Jiang

TL;DR: CoTS根据推理步骤中最可能与次可能令牌之间的概率竞争程度，连续调节离散令牌承诺与软表示探索，无需训练、手调阈值或额外前向。

Details

Motivation: 潜在推理能以连续软嵌入保留多种推理路径，但不同步骤对潜在介入的需求不同：确定时适合离散提交，模糊时应保留备选。既有方法通常采用全局软激活或按不确定度阈值硬切换，形成二元决策。

Result: 竞争令牌选择（CoTS）以两个最高概率候选之间的竞争为信号，在每个推理步骤连续分配潜在介入强度：若一个候选明显占优，则表示靠近采样离散令牌以维持推理轨迹稳定；竞争增强时，逐渐增加软表示的贡献以保留可信替代项。该方法无需人工调整切换阈值或混合系数，也不增加前向计算。六个推理基准实验显示，CoTS持续优于标准思维链和先进免训练潜在推理方法。

Insight: 逐步适应的不确定度介入能在稳定提交与备选探索之间折中，避免全局开关策略。摘要未给模型、基准名称及具体准确率增幅，也没有报告软表示生成和显存开销。

Abstract: Recent latent reasoning methods move beyond discrete Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by replacing sampled tokens with continuous soft embeddings or hidden states, enabling richer intermediate representations and preserving alternative reasoning paths. However, the need for latent reasoning varies across decoding steps: confident predictions favor discrete commitment, whereas ambiguous steps benefit from preserving multiple plausible alternatives. Existing approaches typically handle this variation through either global soft activation or hard switching based on uncertainty thresholds, treating latent intervention as an all-or-nothing decision rather than continuously adapting its strength. To address this limitation, we propose Competitive Token Selection (CoTS), a training-free framework that reframes latent intervention from binary mode selection into continuous allocation at the token level. CoTS uses the probability competition between the two most likely candidates to dynamically balance discrete commitment and soft exploration at each reasoning step. The second candidate represents the strongest alternative to the model’s preferred continuation, so the separation between the two directly reflects the stability of the current discrete choice. When one candidate clearly dominates, CoTS keeps the representation close to the sampled discrete token to preserve a stable reasoning trajectory; as the competition strengthens, it increases the contribution of the soft representation to retain plausible alternatives. This competition-based allocation requires neither manually tuned switching thresholds nor mixing coefficients, and introduces no additional forward passes. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that CoTS consistently outperforms standard CoT and state-of-the-art training-free latent reasoning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/OkinoYu/CoTSReasoning.

[4] PRISM: Transfer and Limits of Activation Interventions in Tool-Calling Language Models cs.CLPDF

Giovana Kerche Bonás, Thales Sales Almeida, Marcelo da Silva Reis, Rodrigo Nogueira, Marcos Meideiros Raimundo

TL;DR: PRISM评估工具调用语言模型中的激活干预能否跨精度、语言和模型规模迁移，并利用NF4量化模型的行为标签及多语数据发现和缓解不安全工具调用。

Details

Motivation: 模型可能一边用文本拒绝，一边仍发出不安全工具调用，形成隐蔽安全漏洞；研究考察激活干预在不同精度、语言和模型规模之间的迁移性。

Result: PRISM发现，利用低资源设置，尤其是NF4量化标签和跨语言迁移，可揭示并改善模型对齐。即使场景数很少，NF4副本生成的行为标签也能稳定估计干预方向；用五种语言NF4监督得到的方向可迁移到未见阿拉伯语，在Llama-3.1-8B上无需目标语言调优便使不安全违规率降低21.95个百分点。通过配对良性激活映射，干预还可迁移至更大模型，最高覆盖70B参数规模。

Insight: 量化副本及多语言辅助监督可作为低成本信号，帮助估计安全干预并跨语言、规模复用；评价应关注实际工具调用行为，而非仅文本拒绝。摘要没有给各精度模型、违规类型和误拒率细项，21.95个百分点对应阿拉伯语、Llama-3.1-8B的报告设置。

Abstract: Recent literature shows that unsafe tool calls can often accompany textual refusals in language models. To investigate this hidden vulnerability, we introduce PRISM, a framework for evaluating the transfer of activation interventions across precision, languages, and scales. Our findings demonstrate that leveraging lower-resource settings (specifically quantized NF4 labels and cross-lingual transfer) offers a robust pathway to expose and improve model alignment. Notably, using behavioral labels from an NF4 copy enables stable intervention estimation even with very few scenarios. Furthermore, these alignment improvements transfer effectively across languages: five-language NF4-supervised directions successfully transfer to held-out Arabic, reducing unsafe violations in Llama-3.1-8B by 21.95 percentage points without target-language tuning. By mapping paired benign activations, we also successfully transfer these safety interventions to much larger models (up to 70B parameters). Ultimately, PRISM highlights how auxiliary supervision from compressed models and multilingual data can uncover and mitigate hazardous tool-calling behaviors across different model families. We release all experimental code and supporting artifacts at https://github.com/GiKerche/prism, alongside an interactive explorer of recorded model responses and experimental results at https://gikerche.github.io/prism/.

[5] Prune the Summary, Not the History: Source-Judged Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents cs.CLPDF

Guanghui Min, Chen Chen

TL;DR: SJC对智能体压缩摘要逐条检查是否可追溯到环境输出或先前摘要，并删除无来源条目，旨在防止压缩器自行推理被误当作环境事实。

Details

Motivation: 长时程智能体把超窗交互历史压成自然语言摘要，但摘要常混合环境确立的事实与压缩器写入的推理，后续智能体无法区分二者；作者对一种常用结构化压缩器的分析发现，83%的失败运行可追溯到执行了摘要中的特定条目，且多数缺少环境输出支持。

Result: Source-Judged Compaction（SJC）是免训练后处理，为每条摘要内容判断是否有环境输出或上一版摘要作为来源，并删除无依据条目；不修改智能体或压缩器，每次压缩仅增加一次低成本判断请求。判定器Jev是非生成式决策模型，输出校准概率而非解释文本。在AppWorld和OfficeBench、两种智能体模型上的实验中，SJC较原压缩器将跨运行可靠性提高2.1%至8.9%，且不增加智能体令牌用量。

Insight: 压缩摘要需保留来源可追溯性，避免模型自行推理被后续当成已核验事实；逐条来源检查可以作为低干预的安全层。83%是对一种压缩器失败运行的归因分析，不代表所有失败类型；摘要未给可靠性指标定义和判断器误删率。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents rely on context compaction: when the interaction history outgrows the context window, a compressor replaces it with a natural-language summary. We show that these summaries contain more than a record of what happened. Compaction prompts ask for decisions and next steps, so each summary mixes facts established by the environment with reasoning written by the compressor, and the agent cannot tell the two apart afterwards. In an analysis of a widely used structured compressor, 83% of failed runs trace back to an action that carries out a specific summary entry, most of which no environment output supports. We propose Source-Judged Compaction (SJC), a training-free post-processing step that scores each summary entry for whether it has a source in the environment outputs or the previous summary, and removes the entries that do not. It changes neither the agent nor the compressor and adds one inexpensive judge request per compaction. The judge is Jev, a non-generative decision model that answers the source question with a calibrated probability instead of text. On AppWorld and OfficeBench with two agent models, SJC improves cross-run reliability over the underlying compressor by 2.1%-8.9% without increasing the agent’s token usage.

[6] CoTranslator: Reverse to Forward Chain-of-Thought Translator for Reasoning Distillation cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Zinan Tang, Zhuoshi Pan, Kairui Chen, Ze Yan, Shaomian Zheng

TL;DR: CoTranslator学习把条件于已知答案生成的反向思维链，翻译成接近经拒绝采样验证的正向推理分布，以降低数学推理蒸馏中的教师调用成本并改善学生表现。

Details

Motivation: 从问题正向生成思维链并拒绝错误答案可获得经验证数据，但难题可能需要大量教师调用；条件于已知答案的反向生成成本较低，却可能事后合理化，而且学生推理时没有答案可用，存在训练—推理不匹配。

Result: CoTranslator用少量配对的反向和经拒绝采样的正向回答训练分布翻译器，再大规模生成并验证学生训练数据。六个学生模型、六个数学基准评测中，平均准确率相较语义抑制方法最高提高5.82个百分点。对两种4B学生，该方法保留拒绝采样平均准确率的95.0%，但估算计算量只需其35.9%。无需重训练，翻译器可迁移到新的数学数据集、不同教师及STEM问题；4B和8B学生的平均STEM准确率分别提高4.88和4.31个百分点。

Insight: 少量成对数据可将低成本、答案条件化的反向推理转换为更贴近学生无答案推理分布的训练样本，兼顾成本与覆盖。35.9%是估算计算成本，摘要未给拒绝采样量、翻译错误率或各基准单项结果。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation requires chain-of-thought (CoT) data with correct answers, but obtaining such data efficiently remains challenging. Forward CoT generation (FCG) followed by rejection sampling (RS) produces verified, question-only responses, yet may require many teacher calls for difficult problems. Reverse CoT generation (RCG) is cheaper because it conditions on the known answer, but access to this answer can lead to post-hoc rationalization and a mismatch with the student’s answer-free inference setting. We propose CoTranslator, which learns to translate reverse CoT responses toward the distribution of verified forward responses. Trained on a small set of paired reverse and rejection-sampled forward responses, it supports large-scale generation and verification of student training data. Across six student models evaluated on six mathematics benchmarks, CoTranslator improves average accuracy over semantic suppression by up to $5.82$ percentage points. For two 4B students, it retains $95.0%$ of rejection sampling’s average accuracy while using $35.9%$ of its estimated compute. Without retraining, the same translator transfers to a new mathematics dataset, a different teacher, and STEM problems, improving average STEM accuracy by $4.88$ and $4.31$ percentage points for 4B and 8B students, respectively. These results show that CoTranslator offers an efficient and transferable approach to reasoning distillation.

[7] Can LLMs Anticipate Their Cruxes? cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

David Reber, James Reber, Ari Holtzman, Victor Veitch

TL;DR: 该研究把模型决策的crux定义为不同推理轨迹意见分歧、且改变答案可能改变结论的子问题，并比较先验猜测、单次推理抽取和多次轨迹对比三种识别成本。

Details

Motivation: 决策辅助需要理解模型为何推荐某个行动，但冗长解释不便使用；关键争点（crux）应揭示哪些不确定子问题若改变答案会影响结论，而不能只从单条推理文本中任意提取。

Result: 作者比较三种计算成本递增的方法：预先让模型判断决策可能的crux；生成一次回答后从推理中抽取实际出现的crux；生成多条回答并比较推理来识别分歧因素。最后一种方法使用低成本重排器，估计计算量比裁判模型低86倍。跨领域和模型实验发现，先验猜测与事后识别的重要因素关系较小；单条轨迹有帮助但仍不足以覆盖完整crux集合。因此，对高风险决策及理解模型决策空间而言，多轨迹比较不可避免。

Insight: crux是模型多次推理分布上的属性，而不是单一回答的解释；多次轨迹对比更能捕捉会改变结论的分歧因素。摘要没有列出领域、识别质量数值和总成本，86倍是相较裁判模型的估算计算差异。

Abstract: To use language models to help us make decisions we would like to understand the rationale behind the language model’s outputs. In principle, the language model provides its rationale as part of its text output. However, the sheer volume of text makes this hard to use in practice. In this paper, we are interested in extracting the cruxes that lead a model to recommend one action over another. We take a crux to be a sub-question that a model’s rollouts disagree on, where changing its value may have altered the conclusion. A crux is therefore a property of the distribution of a model’s rollouts, not of any one rollout. We consider three strategies of increasing computational cost. First, we just ask a model to identify, a priori, what the cruxes of a decision will be. Second, we have the model actually go through the process of generating a response and then extract the cruxes that arose in its reasoning. Finally, we generate many responses and extract the cruxes by comparing the reasoning across the rollouts. We make this last procedure computationally tractable with cheap calls to a reranker, an estimated 86 times less compute than a judge. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation to assess whether the computationally inexpensive options are sufficient. They are not. Across domains and models, we find that a model’s a priori assessment of the cruxes has little relation to the factors that are actually identified as important a posteriori. Using a single rollout helps, but is still inadequate for identifying the full set of cruxes. Accordingly, for high-stakes decisions (and to understand the space of language model decision making) the many rollout procedure is unavoidable.

[8] ReviewPilot: A Multi-Agent System for Screening and Data Extraction in Systematic Reviews cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.IRPDF

Zhaoqian Xue, Shaoze Zhou, Xuexin Li, Junting Mu, Haozheng Liu

TL;DR: ReviewPilot以对话方式配置PRISMA系统综述筛选条件与抽取模式，逐项记录筛选理由和抽取来源，并在流行病学专家综述上超过单提示模型及AgentSLR。

Details

Motivation: 大语言模型深度研究工具能检索和总结文献，但通常不记录哪些研究被排除，也不说明抽取数值来自文中何处，降低系统综述流程的可审计性。

Result: ReviewPilot是对话式多智能体系统，自动执行从文献筛选到信息抽取的PRISMA流程；研究者可通过对话定制筛选标准和字段模式，项目记忆保存设置及其修改。系统为每个筛选决定记录理由，为每个抽取值记录来源。在流行病学专家综述上，跨三种语言模型评测时，它超过单提示LLM和AgentSLR，筛选macro-F1最高0.776、抽取F1最高0.788。生物医学和人机交互案例研究显示其与人工标注者较一致，提示可迁移到流行病学之外。

Insight: 系统综述自动化需要保留逐条排除理由和抽取证据来源，才能支持核查与修订；对话配置和项目记忆便于复用审查规则。摘要未提供纳入研究数量、人工一致性数值及外领域系统性评测规模。

Abstract: Systematic literature reviews follow the PRISMA process of screening scholarly works and extracting evidence from included studies. Deep research tools based on large language models (LLMs) can search and summarize the literature but do not record which studies are excluded or where extracted values come from. We develop a conversational multi-agent system called ReviewPilot that automates the PRISMA workflow from paper screening to information extraction. Researchers can tailor criteria and schemas through dialogue, and a project memory keeps their configurations and revisions. Each screening decision comes with its reason and each extracted value with its source. On epidemiological expert reviews, ReviewPilot outperforms a single-prompt LLM and AgentSLR across three LLMs, with screening macro F1 up to 0.776 and extraction F1 up to 0.788. Case studies in biomedicine and human-computer interaction show close agreement with human annotators, suggesting that ReviewPilot can transfer beyond epidemiology. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhaoqianXue/ReviewPilot.

[9] Multimodal AI Detection for Math-Heavy Scientific Text cs.CLPDF

Yixuan Huang, Danish Pruthi, Bhuwan Dhingra

TL;DR: 该研究发现仅使用PDF提取文本的AI写作检测器在数学内容增多时准确率下降，而利用保留版式信息的LaTeX或页面截图训练，可显著改善证明文本检测。

Details

Motivation: 数学密集型科学文本包含丰富的视觉格式信息，但仅分析PDF抽取文本的检测器无法利用这些布局线索，可能在证明和公式内容较多时失效。

Result: 作者从2022年前的arXiv LaTeX源文件中选取人工撰写的证明和散文，并在同一论文语境下使用15个现代大语言模型重新生成，形成LaTeX源、PDF渲染图像和抽取文本三种配对表示。最先进检测器Pangram 4在数学内容增多时准确率显著下降，文本输入尤其明显。针对该领域微调Qwen-3.5-27B检测器可恢复差距，LaTeX输入明显优于文本。考虑到实际很少能获得LaTeX，作者另训练截图检测器SnapJudge：在0.1%假阳性率下，对前沿模型生成证明和散文的检测率分别为92%和93%，显著超过Pangram 4和文本检测器。

Insight: 检测数学文本来源时保留排版结构或直接使用页面图像，能补足纯文本丢失的视觉信号；极低假阳性率下的检测率也更适合实际审查。结果基于特定合成数据构造与15个生成模型，摘要未给跨领域误报分布及真实混合作者文本的表现。

Abstract: As large language models become increasingly capable of generating, and assisting with, scientific writing, there is a need for greater transparency and disclosure around the use of AI in research. Consequently, a veritable subfield of research and a number of commercial services aim to flag AI-generated content automatically. However, existing detectors which rely only on text extracted from PDFs fail to benefit from the rich visual information contained in the formatting of math-heavy scientific content. To study this, we select human-written proof and prose passages from pre-2022 arXiv LaTeX sources and re-generate them using 15 modern LLMs within the context of the same paper. This leaves us with coupled human and AI outputs in three different representations: the LaTeX source, a rendered image of the PDF, and the text extracted from the PDF. We find that the accuracy of the state-of-the-art Pangram 4 drops significantly as the mathematical content in a passage increases, especially when evaluated on text as opposed to LaTeX. However, this is not an inherent limitation of detecting AI-generated math: finetuning Qwen-3.5-27B based detectors on this domain recovers the gap, with LaTeX inputs performing significantly better than text since they preserve more formatting information. Since LaTeX sources are rarely available in practice, we also train SnapJudge: an image-based detector operating on screenshots of both prose and proofs from papers. At 0.1% false positive rate, SnapJudge achieves a detection rate of 92% on proofs and 93% on prose generated by frontier models, substantially outperforming both Pangram 4 and our text-based detector. We release our data, models and code at https://nlplab.cs.duke.edu/snapjudge.

[10] A Jury, Not an Electorate: Label-Free Reliability Estimation for Ensembles of Language Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.MAPDF

Alessandro Rozza, Davide Mambelli, Steven Motta, Juri Taresch

TL;DR: 该研究将无标签语言模型集成视为由可靠性未知的证人组成的陪审团，用EM根据模型间答案同异估计专家权重，而无需人工标签拟合权重。

Details

Motivation: 集成系统的核心不只是看多数模型支持哪个答案，还需判断各专家是否可靠；现有方法多使用固定信誉或从标注数据学习权重，本文考察能否从无标签模型回答配置本身估计可靠性。

Result: 模型化假设是每题有潜在真实答案，各专家以未知可靠率作答；作者仅根据无标签回答配置用期望最大化估计可靠度，并证明最终决策规则等价于对专家答案分布作log-odds加权线性评分。在1,560题基准上，使用9个最多3B参数专家的陪审团，表现与同一融合规则使用标签拟合权重相当，监督未带来统计显著优势；无标签陪审团还超过匹配自一致性的最佳单模型和最强无标签基线。

Insight: 若不同专家的同异结构足以估计可靠性，集成可以减少对标注权重的依赖；投票规则本身不变，关键变化是信任度的学习方式。结果限于1,560题和9个模型组成，摘要未给具体分数、问题类别或EM估计的不确定性。

Abstract: An ensemble of language models is a jury, not an electorate. The answers are testimony from witnesses of unknown reliability, and the question is not what the majority said but who is credible. Most ensemble methods assign each expert a fixed reputation, often learned from labels. We ask whether it can instead be inferred from the pattern of agreement and disagreement among the experts. We model the experts as a jury: each question has a latent true answer, each expert reports it with unknown reliability, and those reliabilities are estimated by Expectation Maximisation (EM) from unlabelled answer configurations alone. We show that its decision rule reduces to a log-odds weighted linear score over the experts’ answer distributions. What changes is not how the jury votes, but how it learns whom to trust. On a 1560-question benchmark, the jury, with nine experts of up to 3B parameters, performs comparably to the same fusion rule with label-fitted weights, showing no statistically significant advantage from supervision. Instead, it outperforms the best single model with matched self-consistency and the strongest label-free alternative on the full benchmark.

[11] From Observation to Intervention: Distilling Self-Generated In-Context Demonstrations via Executable Causal Intervention cs.CLPDF

Minming Ye, Min Liu, Haoang Chi, Ciheng Wu, Naiyang Guan

TL;DR: ECI2D把自生成示范中的自然语言理由抽象为可执行程序，并在逻辑相似样本上实施因果干预，仅保留表现具有迁移不变性的示范。

Details

Motivation: 自生成上下文学习可让模型自行合成少样本示范，但既有筛选常只看是否得到正确答案；忠实因果理由和事后编造理由可能产生相同输出，单凭观察结果无法区分。

Result: 作者提出可执行因果干预示范蒸馏（ECI2D），将语言理由转为程序，并用do算子在逻辑相似样本上实施干预；若示范蕴含的理由跨样本保持有效则保留。数学、逻辑和常识推理基准实验显示，ECI2D以更少示范持续超过基于观察筛选的上下文学习；测试分布发生变化时，收益超过7个百分点。

Insight: 示范筛选应检验理由能否支持相似问题上的因果迁移，而不是仅检查单个答案是否正确。摘要未列各基准名称和数据规模，也未给出相似样本构造与可执行程序的覆盖范围。

Abstract: Self-generated In-Context Learning (ICL) enables LLMs to synthesize their own few-shot demonstrations, often outperforming manual prompts. However, existing methods filter demonstrations by observation, assuming a rationale is high-quality if it leads to the correct output. We show such filtering suffers from \textit{observational equivalence}, whereby both faithful causal rationales and spurious post-hoc rationales can yield same outputs and are statistically indistinguishable from observation alone. Inspired by independent causal mechanisms, we argue that faithful rationales imply \textit{transfer invariance}, in which causal rationales should remain effective across logically similar samples. We instantiate this criterion as \textbf{E}xecutable \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{I}ntervention for \textbf{D}emonstrations \textbf{D}istillation (ECI2D), which abstracts language rationales into executable programs and applies the causal interventions ($do$-operator) on logically similar samples, retaining only those demonstrations that imply transfer invariance. Experiments on math, logical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ECI2D consistently outperforms observation-based ICL while retaining far fewer demonstrations, with gains exceeding 7 points as the test distribution shifts.

[12] What Should We Remove? Evaluating the Reliability and Costs of RLVR Data Decontamination cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Esther Xin

TL;DR: 该审计比较16个开放RLVR数学语料的八种去污染策略，指出较宽松语义相似度阈值会误删大量样本；经裁判仲裁的策略在其候选范围内具有更好的精确率—召回率权衡。

Details

Motivation: RLVR数学语料常按与基准题重叠进行清洗，但清洗规则的可靠性和数据损失成本研究不足，仅报告重叠数量不能说明清洗是否误删无关训练样本。

Result: 作者审计16个开放语料、共1,673,514条语料内训练样本，对照六个基准中的1,949道题，评估基于精确、词汇和语义匹配的八种策略，并盲审594组训练—基准配对。校准集和留出集在基准题及训练样例族上互不重叠；另对80个训练样本的83个候选匹配进行误删审查。在余弦阈值0.80下，抽样移除的59项中52项（88%）被证实为误删，但这不是总体误删率。预注册0.80—0.98语义阈值均未在校准集达到90%保守精确率目标。现有裁判仲裁策略在留出语料上的已裁定精确率98.1%、候选框架召回率98.2%；六个抽样移除中无已确认误删，但另有一例未决。作者明确指出这不证明风险为零，且该召回率不衡量候选框架之外的污染。

Insight: 数据去污染需同时审计精确率、召回率和误删成本，并明确候选匹配范围；宽松语义阈值可能造成大量误删。裁判策略的高指标仅适用于评估的候选框架和校准/留出条件，不能推断未知污染或零误删。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) math corpora are routinely screened for benchmark overlap, but the reliability and data-loss costs of cleaning policies remain underexamined. We audit sixteen open RLVR math corpora comprising 1,673,514 corpus-specific training items against six benchmarks containing 1,949 questions. We evaluate eight policies based on exact, lexical, and semantic matching using 594 independently and blindly dual-reviewed training-benchmark pairs. Calibration and held-out sets have disjoint benchmark questions and training-example families. A separate review of all 83 candidate matches for 80 sampled training items measures item-level false removal. At cosine threshold 0.80, 52 of 59 sampled removals (88%) were confirmed false removals; this is not a demonstrated population rate. No semantic threshold in our preregistered grid (0.80-0.98) meets the 90% conservative-precision target on calibration. The existing judge-adjudicated policy achieves 98.1% resolved precision and 98.2% candidate-frame recall on held-out corpora. It has zero confirmed false removals among six sampled removals, with one unresolved case; this does not establish zero risk. Within the evaluated candidate frame, this policy offers a more favorable precision-recall balance than the semantic-threshold alternatives tested. Recall does not measure contamination outside the candidate frame. We release reviewed labels, per-policy decisions, and coverage accounting to support evaluation of cleaning reliability and cost rather than overlap counts alone.

Tianyu Chen, Xinyang Liu, Ruyi An, Zhendong Wang, Mingyuan Zhou

Motivation: 现实应用中的模型常要在离散选项中快速决策；System 1可一次前向输出选项概率，而System 2生成推理过程。需要厘清哪些静态或交互决策能由轻量概率模型处理、哪些需要生成式推理。

Result: 基准包含来自268个公开来源的92,000题和105项任务，分为文本分类与知识、应用决策、评估与控制、智能体与行动四组；另含138个闭环环境，覆盖网页购物、SWE-bench代码控制、机器人、游戏和驾驶，实时设置中环境会在模型决策时继续演变。作者评测30个模型：7个闭源LLM、13个在答案令牌处读取的开源LLM和10个System-1模型。最大的System-1模型在非智能体任务上可匹配或超过快速闭源LLM，概率校准更好且延迟约为四分之一；推理LLM最准确，但成本约为快速LLM的70倍。闭环任务中大型开源LLM成功最多（最高32.9%），小型System-1模型很少成功。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often make discrete decisions, such as flagging phishing or escalating to a human. System-1 models, such as Jev and the OpenAI Decisions API, return probabilities over options in a single forward pass, while System-2 approaches generate reasoning steps. Which decisions can System-1 models handle, and which require generative reasoning? We introduce System1-Arena, a benchmark that gives all models the same decision requests and scores their probabilities. It contains 92k questions across 105 tasks from 268 public sources, in four groups: text classification and knowledge, applied decisions, evaluation and control, and agent and action. It also includes 138 closed-loop environments, from web shopping and SWE-bench coding control to robotics, games, and driving; in real-time mode, the world evolves while a model decides. We evaluate 30 models: 7 closed LLMs, 13 open LLMs read at the answer token, and 10 System-1 models. The largest System-1 models match or exceed fast closed LLMs in non-agentic accuracy, with better-calibrated probabilities at one-quarter of the latency. A reasoning LLM is the most accurate, at about 70x the cost of a fast LLM. In closed loops, large open LLMs succeed most often (up to 32.9%); small System-1 models rarely succeed.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-guided Bayesian optimization (BO) uses past observations to guide search, but proposed directions and their revision can remain implicit. We propose Feedback-Guided Search State (FGSS), a structured hypothesis-management interface connecting language-based guidance to observed evidence, executable parameter interventions, and comparative support updates. An LLM reflector proposes and revises hypotheses; a deterministic curator validates them and maintains identities and test records. The evolving state guides candidate construction and priorities. Instantiated on LLAMBO, FGSS-BO retains its surrogate and default expected improvement selection, with a bounded testing policy. Result: 自目标内部注意力增强（SIAB）先在合成最小对上通过因果定位（具体为激活置换）识别稀疏情绪头，再于答案位置增强这些头原本关注的内容令牌；头部和目标令牌均由模型自身行为确定，同一头集合可复用于不同任务。在三个大语言模型、多种提示设置以及ECC和GoEmotions评测中，SIAB较匹配提示基线最高提升ECC宏F1 10.3分、GoEmotions宏F1 5.5分。与PASTA式外部监督结合时，准确率最高提高13.84个百分点、宏F1提高12.51分。机制分析将收益关联到稀疏且富含情绪信息的注意力头，并显示层位置会影响组合增益。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly operate in multi-turn interactions that require maintaining latent conversational states, not only explicit facts. We propose Self-targeted Internal Attention Boosting (SIAB), which localizes sparse affective heads via causal localization (in detail activation patching) on synthetic minimal pairs and boosts, at the answer position, the content tokens these heads already attend to; both edited heads and target tokens are derived from the model’s own behavior, enabling the same head set to be reused across tasks.We evaluate on ECC and GoEmotions across three LLMs and multiple prompting settings. Abstract: Distribution shifts pose a fundamental challenge to robust multimodal learning. Existing methods typically promote invariance by suppressing environment-dependent variations, implicitly applying a uniform treatment to shifted information. However, shifted information can have different predictive consequences: some contributes little to prediction, some remains reliably informative, and some exhibits environment-dependent predictive effects. This raises a fundamental question: which shifted information should be suppressed, preserved, or attenuated? To address this question, we propose FaShift, a factor-wise shift framework that diagnoses latent representation factors according to their distributional changes, intervention effects, and cross-environment stability. By factorizing multimodal representations and evaluating these complementary properties, FaShift distinguishes non-predictive factors, stably predictive factors, and predictively useful but unstable factors. It then selectively suppresses, preserves, or attenuates their contributions, rather than enforcing uniform invariance. Experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and CH-SIMS show that FaShift achieves the best ID accuracy and F1 across all three datasets. Compared with the strongest competitors reporting OOD results, it improves OOD accuracy/F1 by 1.5/3.6 points on MOSI and 1.7/2.7 points on MOSEI, while achieving the best reported OOD accuracy and F1 on CH-SIMS.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves multimodal reasoning, but group-relative policy optimization broadcasts a single sequence-level advantage to every token of a response, although errors usually localize to a few decisive steps. Recent self-distilled variants refine credit with a teacher–student likelihood gap. However, this signal cannot tell whether it relies on the image or on a language shortcut, and is applied uniformly even when the teacher is uninformative. To address this, we propose Pairwise token contrast and Adaptive trajectory ConfidencE (PACE). Pairwise token contrast scores each response under four teacher views, crossing a correct or incorrect answer hint with the image present or removed, and compares the two hints for every token, which cancels shifts shared by both hints and reveals whether the token’s support relies on the image. Adaptive trajectory confidence measures how well the teacher predicts each response given the correct hint and the image, normalizes this score within the batch, and uses it to set how far the token contrast redistributes credit within that response. PACE achieves the best average accuracy on three backbones and ranks first on all evaluated multimodal reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Experiments show that both components matter: replacing pairwise token contrast with a single-view signal underperforms our method, while adaptive trajectory confidence adds further improvement.

[24] Beyond Context Length: Characterising the Difficulty of Long-Context Reasoning cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: LURCH把长上下文推理难度拆为证据范围、证据分散度和推理深度，并提供6,550条带支持证据及推理图的多领域问答实例。

Details

Motivation: 上下文窗口长度常被当作长上下文任务难度指标，但同等长度的材料可能只需阅读一段局部内容，也可能需要整合分散证据并完成多步依赖推理。

Result: 作者用三个属性定量描述难度：找到答案所需信息量的证据范围、信息在上下文中的分布广度，以及从证据到答案所需的依赖推理步数。LURCH包含6,550个多领域问答实例，并提供支持证据与推理图以测量这些属性。专有及开放权重模型评估显示，在固定上下文长度下，三项属性均与模型表现强负相关；上下文变长时，证据分散样本的性能急剧下降，而局部证据样本相对稳定。三类难度因素会复合：三者均中等偏高的问题比仅一项极高的问题更难。

Insight: 长上下文推理难度不仅由token长度决定，还取决于需查找多少证据、证据相隔多远及推理链有多深；基准应报告这些任务结构。摘要未给各模型的具体表现和属性测量一致性。

Abstract: Long-context language models support ever longer context windows, and evaluations typically treat context length as the main indicator of difficulty. However, context length alone cannot explain why models fail on long-context tasks that require reasoning over complex information. Contexts of equal length can pose very different challenges: a task may be solvable from a single local passage, or it may require integrating evidence distributed across the full context. We quantitatively characterise this difficulty through three properties: evidence scope, how much necessary information must be found; evidence dispersion, how widely that information is scattered within the context; and reasoning depth, how many dependent steps are needed to derive an answer from it. We construct LURCH, a multi-domain question-answering benchmark of 6,550 instances with supporting evidence and reasoning graphs, enabling all three properties to be quantified. Evaluations of proprietary and open-weight models show that: (1) at fixed context length, all our three properties are strongly negatively correlated with model performance; (2) as context length increases, performance degrades sharply on samples with dispersed evidence, whereas it remains relatively stable on samples with localised evidence; and (3) difficulty compounds across properties, as questions with moderately high values on all three are harder than those with an extreme value on just one. These findings establish evidence scope, dispersion and reasoning depth as measures of long-context reasoning difficulty that complement context length.

Generating a free-form response for each choice adds latency and complicates the interface between a model and its environment. We present Intern-Decision, a family of 0.8B, 2B, and 4B multimodal decision models that return structured choices and candidate probabilities from text and optional images. Our autoregressive masked language modeling objective places one decision placeholder per field in a complete assistant JSON skeleton. Ground-truth symbols appear only in supervision, while all fields are evaluated in a single causal forward pass using the backbone’s native language-model head. The 4B model achieves a 90.02% unweighted average accuracy across seven evaluation suites, compared with 88.74% for Jev v1.13.0. For a fixed 289-token request with three fields on one RTX 4090, mean latency is 44.16 ms for the 4B model and approximately 33–34 ms for the smaller models. On a separate 96-case known-distribution pilot, temperature scaling reduces expected multiclass Brier from 0.628 to 0.550 and expected calibration error from 0.213 to 0.089. A confidence-based handoff study on 111 public Jevbench-Hard items improves accuracy from 82/111 (73.87%) to 97/111 (87.39%) by routing 42 items to Atria-Dawn-Preview. We also describe qualitative game, visual interface, and browser demonstrations. These results support compact decision models as a practical component of larger agent systems.

[26] EMO: A Closed-Loop Test-Time Computation Framework via Alternating Context Optimization and Logits Steering cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Wei Xia, Yehui Tang, Zhi-Hong Deng

Motivation: 测试时上下文优化可用文本反馈适配冻结模型，但普通条件生成无法单独控制上下文更新导致的分布变化强度。

Insight: 上下文编辑与令牌分布干预被作为两个相互反馈的控制环节，理论保证依赖上下文改善和引导忠实等假设。候选覆盖—选择差距提示测试时计算还需联合分配给生成与验证；覆盖更高并不意味着最终所选答案必然更好。摘要未列八模型名称和39种设置的逐项分数。

Abstract: Test-time context optimization adapts frozen language models through textual feedback, but ordinary conditional generation provides no separate control over how strongly to reinforce the distributional changes induced by a context update. We introduce EMO, a training-free framework inspired by expectation–maximization that alternates question-specific context estimation (E-step) and distribution refinement (M-step). The E-step revises a system context using textual gradients from an external judge without access to gold answers. The M-step contrasts next-token distributions under the revised and preceding contexts at a shared prefix to construct a confidence-constrained logit correction. Feedback on the steered response drives the next context update, coupling adaptation across rounds with intervention within each generation. Theoretically, we connect parameter and context optimization through a shared target-KL objective and additive logit representations, and derive the steering vector from context-induced logit displacements. Under context-improvement and faithful-steering assumptions, we prove a one-round decrease in target KL divergence for sufficiently small steering strength. Under greedy decoding across eight models and six benchmarks, EMO improves the zero-shot baseline in 39 of 40 aggregated settings, averaging $+9.10$ percentage points. With the same judge, it adds $+1.53$ points on average over context-only optimization (TextGrad), with consistent MMLU-Pro gains across models. At matched target-model generation budgets, accuracy advantages over best-of-$N$ are strongest on Llama, while oracle coverage is higher in most comparisons despite fewer candidates. The coverage–selection gap motivates allocating test-time computation jointly to candidate generation and verification.

Abstract: Understanding a conversation requires connecting what is said to the speakers’ relationships, expectations, and earlier exchanges. Prior work shows that supplying relevant context can improve models’ social judgments, but much of this information may already be present in the conversation. This motivates training models to discover the cues that matter and use them to support an interpretation. We propose CLUE, a two-stage training pipeline that combines supervised evidence reasoning with GRPO guided by Grounded Information Utilization ($\mathrm{IU}_{\mathrm{G}}$). This reward credits new, evidence-supported social inferences while accounting for reasoning effort. We train on a small set of conversational examples to assess effectiveness and generalization across tasks, languages, and genres. Motivation: 多数智能体RL假定轨迹始终在一个累积上下文中运行，但上下文压缩和子智能体分叉会产生跨多个会话的推理轨迹；将轨迹级优势广播给所有会话无法区分各会话贡献，也不能指导会话预算分配。

Result: Mosaic是无critic的分层多会话优化方法：轨迹层把会话使用信号纳入优势估计，会话层依据估计的边际贡献塑造各会话优势。在Qwen3.5-4B及BrowseComp上，分别在上下文压缩和子智能体分叉场景中超过将轨迹优势广播到所有会话的基线，且平均使用会话数更少。分析显示会话效用和分配均有改善。

Abstract: Most existing agentic reinforcement learning methods assume single-session trajectories, where interaction proceeds within one continuously accumulated context. However, agentic techniques such as context compaction and subagent forking may produce trajectories spanning multiple sessions, creating a mismatch between the single-session training assumption and inference-time execution. Therefore, we introduce Multi-Session Agentic Reinforcement Learning, a more general setting that supports an arbitrary number of sessions within a trajectory. Under this setting, a widely adopted method is to broadcast trajectory-level advantages to all sessions, but this does not distinguish contributions from various sessions or guide session allocation within trajectories. To address this issue, we propose Mosaic, a multi-session agentic RL method with critic-free hierarchical optimization. At the trajectory level, it incorporates session-usage signals into advantage estimation. At the session level, it shapes each session’s advantage according to the estimated marginal contribution. Experiments on BrowseComp with Qwen3.5-4B show that Mosaic outperforms broadcasting in both context compaction and subagent forking scenarios while using fewer sessions on average. Further analyses show that our method achieves improvements in session utility and allocation, which we suggested is a promising target for future advances in multi-session agentic RL.

Abstract: External-memory systems extend language-model applications beyond a context window, but their quality depends critically on the model executing the write path: deciding what to retain, how precisely to represent it, and how new evidence should update persistent state. Existing frameworks typically place a general-purpose large language model behind framework-specific prompts. This raises serving cost, and a writer specialized for one protocol does not transfer directly to another; a unified model that can operate across established memory frameworks remains largely unexplored. We present Hy-Memory-30B-A3B, a compact long-term-memory writer trained across Mem0, MemOS, and Hy-Memory. Memory On-Policy Distillation teaches framework-specific write protocols on student-generated states; Memory RL then optimizes downstream answerability using dense per-chunk probes, attributed cross-chunk rewards, over-storage controls, and chunk-level GRPO. Details

Motivation: 现有记忆基准以问答准确率代理记忆质量，却把记忆是否正确与检索和生成能力混在一起；正确回答可能掩盖损坏记忆，错误回答也可能把正确记忆的检索失败误判为记忆错误。

Result: FEB-30包含30条事实演变轨迹、6类诊断类别和3个难度等级，通过从已知事实轨迹逆向生成文档，建立无需人工标注的确定真值。每条轨迹规定事实在多作者文档、邮件和对话中的更新方式，可直接检查每次摄取后的记忆库。对代表性记忆系统评测发现，内在记忆检查与外在问答平均相差16个百分点，44%的轨迹超过有意义差异阈值。系统对创建、更新、强化、替代等记忆操作的正确分类率仅20%，而问答评估完全无法发现；失效模式还随类别和难度系统变化。

Insight: 应将记忆库状态演变与下游检索/回答分开评估，避免把错误归因混为一谈；逐次摄取检查能暴露问答正确率掩盖的记忆操作缺陷。基准仅30条合成事实轨迹，外推到真实开放式记忆仍需验证。

Abstract: Agent memory benchmarks evaluate whether an agent’s responses reflect correct knowledge, measuring downstream question-answering accuracy as a proxy for memory quality. We show this evaluation paradigm is incomplete: it conflates memory correctness with retrieval and generation quality, producing both false positives where correct responses mask broken memory and false negatives where incorrect responses blame correct memory for retrieval failures. We introduce FEB-30, a benchmark of 30 fact-evolution trajectories spanning 6 diagnostic categories and 3 difficulty levels, constructed via paradigm inversion: documents are generated from known fact trajectories, yielding deterministic ground truth without human annotation. Each trajectory defines how a fact should evolve across multi-author artifacts (documents, emails, conversations), enabling direct inspection of the memory store after each ingestion step. Applying FEB-30 to a representative memory system, we demonstrate that intrinsic and extrinsic evaluation diverge by an average of 16 percentage points, with 44% of trajectories exceeding meaningful divergence thresholds. Diagnostic decomposition reveals that the system correctly classifies memory operations (create, update, reinforce, supersede) only 20% of the time, a failure entirely invisible to extrinsic evaluation, and that failure modes differ systematically by category and difficulty. We release the benchmark, document generation pipeline, and scoring framework to support reproducible evaluation of agent memory systems.

Abstract: One of the first steps of a research pipeline is generating ideas. Abstract: Generative visual verifiers play a crucial role in advancing multimodal intelligence. To enhance the robustness of visual verifiers in complex multimodal scenarios involving compositional requirements, we move beyond conventional binary verifiers that produce only True/False discrete judgments and introduce a verifier that automatically decomposes complex prompts or environmental rules into fine-grained components and assigns pointwise scores to provide a continuous and more informative critique signal. Our experiments further demonstrate that these structured and interpretable outputs improve the verifier’s generalization to out-of-domain multimodal critic tasks. To train this verifier, we introduce a Decoupled RL training strategy that jointly optimizes sub-question-level binary judgment and global alignment scoring, using precise local feedback to develop fine-grained verification capabilities that provide a foundation for reliable continuous scoring at the global level. Under a general reinforcement learning framework, we first empirically demonstrate that the proposed pointwise graded verifier significantly outperforms binary verifiers in traditional text-to-image scenarios. We then extend our investigation to rule-governed generative world modeling where generative models are leveraged for world simulation and world reasoning under explicit rules. From a theoretical standpoint, we further show that a pairwise verification paradigm yields more accurate critiques than the pointwise formulation when ground-truth images are available, with empirical results on diverse structured world-modeling tasks such as Maze and Sudoku further validating the effectiveness of our approach. Overall, our findings suggest a key insight: the transition from generative models to structured world-modeling agents critically hinges on the availability of accurate pairwise graded visual verifiers.

Abstract: Video generators serve as interactive worlds for exploration and simulation, yet realistic and visually appealing frames do not necessarily depict a consistent 3D world. We introduce Orbia, the first benchmark dedicated to a systematic and comprehensive evaluation of 3D consistency in interactive world models. Through controlled exploration and revisit, we organize the evaluation into three dimensions: input preservation, generated-content persistence, and 3D self-consistency. For input preservation, registered RGB-D references and annotated anchors support appearance, geometry, and spatial stability measurements. Abstract: Humans interpret multimodal observations of the world and forecast how it will evolve. Recent multimodal generative systems have demonstrated similar capabilities: they can integrate multimodal context and synthesize visual outputs that depict changes in the world. To characterize this emerging trajectory, we organize model capabilities into a four-level hierarchy and discover that higher-level tasks remain relatively underexplored despite model progress. To bridge this gap, we explore model performance on multi-step world state prediction, a high-level task that requires models to forecast successive future world states from multimodal observations and diverse references. We introduce WorldCast, a benchmark comprising 320 manually designed cases across eight categories, and evaluate 12 representative models. These findings highlight the need for multimodal generative models that are capable of dynamic planning and reasoning while also being deeply unified to faithfully simulate real-world states.

Details

Motivation: 视觉文本生成仍有错词和畸形字形；识别器式奖励可能漏掉可见字形缺陷，而整图标量反馈又掩盖不同文字区域质量不均的问题。

Result: 作者提出“归责于错误文字，而不是整张画布”的原则。TextDART定位每个文本区域，分别评价语义正确性和字形质量，生成区域级细粒度奖励；SCANNER将各采样图像中同一文字目标的奖励归一化，再把区域优势映射到相应潜在令牌，形成空间差异化策略反馈。在既有基准及新建PolyText-Bench上的实验中，该方法在文字准确性和字形保真度方面达到报告的最先进表现。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning approaches have improved visual text generation, yet generated images still suffer from incorrect wording and malformed glyphs. We identify two coupled limitations: recognition-based rewards often overlook visible glyph defects, while whole-image scalar feedback obscures the uneven quality of individual text regions. These limitations motivate our central principle: $\textit{Blame errant words, not the canvas}$, to effectively preserve local quality distinctions. First, we introduce $\mathbf{TextDART}$, a defect-aware region-targeted reward model that localizes individual text regions and independently evaluates their semantic correctness and glyph quality, providing fine-grained, region-precise feedback. Building on TextDART, we propose $\mathbf{SCANNER}$, which turns these regional judgments into spatially differentiated policy feedback. It normalizes rewards for each text target across sampled images and casts the resulting regional advantages onto the corresponding latent tokens. Extensive experiments on existing benchmarks and our newly established $\textbf{PolyText-Bench}$ demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in text accuracy and glyph fidelity. Project page: https://sujiehu.github.io/textdart-scanner

Abstract: Negation is a fundamental aspect of language understanding and it is important vision-language models (VLMs) are able to process it effectively. Abstract: Unified multimodal generation covers discrete, continuous, and hybrid paradigms. Discrete approaches with single-codebook visual tokenizers align naturally with text modeling but have limited representational capacity. Continuous and hybrid approaches retain richer visual representations, but they model text and images in heterogeneous spaces with different training objectives, potentially introducing optimization conflicts between modalities with misaligned representation spaces. These choices expose a central question: how to efficiently model high-capacity visual representations alongside textual content in a shared optimization space within a single backbone. We present Intern Lumina U2, to our knowledge the first diffusion language model to represent multi-codebook visual tokens in a fully discrete space. Each visual position is represented by a group of codes from complementary codebooks, increasing its representational capacity while preserving compatibility with discrete text modeling. At the input, the embeddings are concatenated at each position; at the output, a lightweight multi-codebook autoregressive head predicts the codes within each position sequentially, while the diffusion backbone processes all positions in parallel. Built on the unified 4D representation of AToken, Intern Lumina U2 supports image, video, and 3D inputs within a single backbone and achieves leading performance among unified models in visual understanding, image generation, and image editing. We further build a full-stack training, inference, and evaluation system on both Ascend and NVIDIA platforms to verify the cross-platform effectiveness of our model. We also improve the inference efficiency through a stability-aware sampling strategy, which combines confidence-plateau-guided unmasking approach with drift-triggered cache refreshing mechanisms, accelerating image generation by 4.45x.

TL;DR: TEAL在冻结ViT原生令牌网格上进行多样性采样，再于解码网格用不确定性细化选择像素标注点，面向极低标注预算的语义分割主动学习。

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉Transformer主动学习常按patch或区域选样，像素级监督仍需大量标注；当每图仅能标注极少像素时，还需同时挑选信息量最大的像素并从稀疏信号训练可靠解码器。

Result: TEAL直接在冻结ViT令牌网格进行多样性选择，避免将令牌嵌入插值到稠密像素空间引入伪影；先对多层令牌描述符进行两级MaxHerding选择代表性候选，再在更细解码网格用边际不确定度进行精炼。在CamVid、Cityscapes、ADE20K和Pascal Context上，极低标签预算下持续超过先前基线：每张图只标1个像素时，较最强基线最高提升24.36 mIoU；每图10个像素时最高达到全监督表现的97%。为区分增益来源，作者还在所有基线统一使用DINOv3骨干时确认提升仍存在。

Insight: 利用ViT令牌几何做样本选择，再结合解码器网格的不确定性，能避免简单上采样令牌后选择像素的失真，并在极少像素标注下保持性能。最高提升及97%表现是不同数据集/设置的上限，不代表每个基准均达到。

Abstract: While foundation models and vision transformers (ViTs) have redefined visual representations, current active learning (AL) strategies for these backbones largely operate at the patch or region level, where annotation requirements remain high due to extensive pixel labeling. Extending ViT-based AL to the pixel-level, low-budget regime is uniquely challenging: with as little as one pixel query per image, a method must simultaneously identify the most informative locations and train a reliable decoder head from an extremely sparse signal. We introduce TEAL (Token-space Efficient Active Learning), the first pixel-level AL framework built around ViT representations. TEAL uses a frozen ViT backbone and performs diversity-based selection directly on its native token lattice, avoiding the artifacts introduced by interpolating token embeddings to dense pixel resolutions. Our framework applies a two-level MaxHerding strategy over multi-layer token descriptors to select representative candidates, followed by margin-based uncertainty refinement on a finer decoder grid. Across CamVid, Cityscapes, ADE20K, and Pascal Context, TEAL consistently outperforms previous baselines under extreme label scarcity, improving over the strongest baseline by up to +24.36 mIoU with a single labeled pixel (px) per image, and reaching up to 97% of fully supervised performance with just 10 px. To rigorously establish the source of the gains, we further show that the improvements persist when all baselines are equipped with the same DINOv3 backbone, demonstrating that coupling token-space selection with uncertainty refinement on the decoder grid makes ViT token geometry an effective foundation for extremely low-budget active segmentation.

Abstract: Existing long-document QA benchmarks rarely combine large-scale human-authored questions with detailed visual evidence annotations, limiting diagnosis of evidence localization and interpretation failures. We present VELD-QA, a large-scale benchmark for Visual Evidence grounding and question answering in Long Documents. It contains 15,000 fully human-authored question-answer pairs across 5,859 real-world PDFs totaling 372,445 pages, balancing chart, table, and complex-layout tasks. Human annotations identify supporting pages and evidence elements and describe cited visual content, enabling separate retrieval and interpretation evaluation. We evaluate nine vision-language models under four evidence settings and seven retrieval-augmented and agentic systems. Our analysis identifies bottlenecks in evidence localization and interpretation. Retrieval-based methods substantially underperform the oracle setting with ground-truth supporting pages; full-document access does not close the gap. Localization alone is insufficient: oracle accuracy peaks at 81.7%, highlighting persistent visual interpretation and reasoning challenges. Result: DeSpatial提供共享3D感知工具，将独立感知结果整合为统一的度量化、重力对齐场景。联合分割—深度协同细化用于合并同一物体的掩码并消除单帧分割和深度不一致导致的投影伪影；跨视角实例融合合并体素重叠实例，但禁止合并同一帧中的不同实例，还根据三维结构和源掩码外观融合因观测不足造成的空间分离碎片。获得完整统一场景后，智能体再选择推理或计算工具。方法在VSI-Bench、MindCube-Tiny和ViewSpatial上取得领先结果，并报告VSI-SUPER长视频结果。

Abstract: Spatial agents have access to reconstruction and segmentation tools, yet inconsistent predictions and fragmented object identities can prevent their outputs from becoming reliable 3D scene information. A simple oracle experiment exposes this perception bottleneck: replacing tool-predicted perception with ground-truth 3D bounding boxes raises an agent to nearly full score across five object-centric VSI-Bench tasks, while retaining the same reasoning tools. Motivated by this finding, we believe the devil of spatial reasoning lies in 3D itself, and introduce DeSpatial(Devil of Spatial), which explicitly decomposes spatial understanding into perception and reasoning. DeSpatial has a shared 3D perception tool to reconcile independent perception outputs into a unified, metric, gravity-aligned 3D scene. This perception tool has two core components: a) a joint segmentation–depth co-refinement module that consolidates masks of the same object and removes projection artifacts caused by inconsistent segmentation and depth within a single frame; b) a cross-view instance fusion module that consolidate voxel-overlapping instances but not allowed to merge same-frame instances. This fusion module additionally fuses spatially disjoint fragments caused by insufficient multi-view observations, by checking their 3D structure and source mask appearance. After receiving the complete and unified 3D scene information, the agent chooses reasoning or computational tools to solve the spatial problem. DeSpatial achieves leading performance on VSI-Bench, MindCube-Tiny, and ViewSpatial, with additional long-video results on VSI-SUPER, revealing the bottleneck of spatial understanding lies in a precise 3D perception itself.

Abstract: Data selection reallocates training support across visual concepts, including objects, attributes, color, texture, and backgrounds. Different allocation strategies may produce varied downstream model behavior, especially for tail concepts supported by only a few samples. Discarding them can hurt test-time robustness, while overemphasizing samples lying on category boundaries may introduce unstable training signals. This work explicitly investigates this relationship across fine-grained, spurious-correlation, and general image classification, as well as multimodal pretraining. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to extract concept activations from individual images. During selection, we accumulate the dataset-level concept support for each added sample. Applying diminishing returns to this distribution down-weights well-represented concepts and shifts the focus to the distribution tail. We then incorporate local support to find samples that reliably strengthen under-supported concepts. Empirical results show consistent improvements over strong data selection baselines across all benchmarks. We also reveal the connection between concepts our method enhances and the induced behavioral changes, including improved robustness against out-of-distribution and spurious contexts. This work establishes concept support, beyond individual sample importance, as a useful principle for data-efficient and robust learning.

Details

Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer的自注意力计算随令牌数平方增长。稀疏注意力能跳过部分计算，但既有方法在整个去噪过程固定稀疏率，且难以准确判断哪些计算可安全跳过。

Result: 作者通过注意力熵和平均注意距离分析去噪动态，发现注意力随去噪逐步集中，早期选中的块在后续仍承载大量注意力权重。GSRS据此前期保留更多计算、后期逐步减少，并将后续搜索限制在早期候选集合内。方法使用Top-p自适应控制稀疏率，并在FlashAttention计算中精确评估注意力权重，额外开销可忽略。HunyuanVideo和Wan 2.1实验显示，加速比为1.55至2.21倍，生成质量与全注意力相当。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) dominate video generation but suffer from high inference cost due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Sparse attention mitigates this by skipping unnecessary computation in the attention matrix, but existing methods apply a fixed sparsity ratio throughout denoising and struggle to accurately determine which computations to skip. To address these limitations, we analyze attention dynamics through attention entropy and mean attention distance, and find that attention becomes increasingly concentrated across denoising steps. We further observe that blocks selected early continue to capture a large fraction of the total attention weight at later steps. These findings suggest two acceleration opportunities: the sparsity ratio should grow as denoising progresses, and earlier search results can be reused to reduce the search space. Based on these findings, we propose Growing Sparsity and Reduced Search (GSRS), a training-free framework that retains more computation early and progressively reduces it later, while restricting subsequent searches to an early candidate set to reduce search overhead. GSRS adopts Top-p selection for adaptive sparsity control, supported by a precise evaluation scheme that computes exact attention weights within the FlashAttention computation at negligible overhead. Experiments on HunyuanVideo and Wan 2.1 validate that GSRS achieves 1.55$\times$–2.21$\times$ speedup while maintaining generation quality comparable to full attention.

Current benchmarks treat both alike, testing whether a model can read the text rather than whether it should trust it. We study claim deference, multimodal large language models’ tendency to let an on-screen claim override conflicting visual evidence. We introduce VisualTrust, a counterfactual benchmark with 5,063 cases across 14 claim-fact classes. Each case pairs a question with three versions of the same video: one with no claim, one with an accurate claim, and one with a misleading claim that alters a single grounded fact. The option the misleading claim supports is the trap answer. Across seven open-weight and four proprietary models, mean accuracy falls from 90.1% without a claim to 51.9% with a misleading claim. Among initially correct responses, 42.8% flip to the trap answer and only 0.6% become other errors. Strong no-claim accuracy does not ensure resistance. We also introduce DEAR (Decoupled Evidence Arbitration and Reading), a training-free agent for video question answering that separates evidence arbitration from reading. An independent check reports conflicting evidence, a selector weighs it without a fixed modality priority and blinds what it withholds, and the reader answers from the resulting video in a fresh context. On 500 videos across three proprietary models, DEAR raises accuracy under misleading claims from 47.2–64.0% to 81.8–85.2% and reduces trap-answer selection from 34.4–50.8% to 11.6–15.0%, while preserving no-claim accuracy.

[51] ControlFlow3D: Distilling Multi-View Knowledge into Latent Flow Matching for Point Cloud Upsampling cs.CVPDF

Yuang Liu, Zhi Zuo, Zhengkai Zhao, Lirui Zhang, Pan Gao

TL;DR: ControlFlow3D在冻结3D编码器定义的结构化潜空间中做条件流匹配，通过多视图图像与3D令牌共享原型逐步蒸馏视觉知识，用于无需测试时多视图图像的点云上采样。

Details

Motivation: 稀疏点云观测欠约束，使高质量上采样困难；多视图图像可以缓解几何歧义，但现有多模态方法未能充分利用视觉线索，且测试时依赖图像会限制部署。

Result: ControlFlow3D在训练阶段把多视图图像令牌和3D潜变量令牌通过无监督软K-means聚为共享原型，再用速度控制引导潜空间流轨迹，并以一致性损失逐步把图像知识迁入基础网络。点云潜空间由冻结3D编码器定义，Mamba几何解码器以线性复杂度将潜在特征映射为稠密坐标；测试时无需多视图图像。作者另构建包含3.5万物体的ShapeNetPU多模态基准，规模为先前数据集的31倍。多个基准实验支持方法有效性及较强泛化。

Insight: 训练期用多视图视觉知识引导潜空间生成、测试期只需点云，可兼顾几何消歧能力和部署简洁性；线性复杂度解码器也针对输出点数扩展。摘要没有具体上采样指标、对照结果或多视图数目，泛化结论只作定性报告。

Abstract: Point cloud upsampling is essential for 3D applications but remains challenging due to sparse, under-constrained observations. Multi-view images can resolve geometric ambiguity, but existing multi-modal methods do not fully exploit visual cues. We propose ControlFlow3D, a framework that distills knowledge from pretrained foundation models into a lightweight generation network during training, enabling strong performance without multi-view images available at test time. Specifically, our method performs conditional flow matching in a structured latent space defined by a frozen 3D encoder. Multi-view image tokens and 3D latent tokens are jointly clustered into shared prototypes via unsupervised soft KMeans; the resulting prototypes guide the flow trajectory through velocity control and are aligned by a consistency loss that progressively transfers image knowledge into the base network. A Mamba-based geometry decoder then maps latent features to dense coordinates with linear complexity. We also construct ShapeNetPU, a 35K-object multi-modal benchmark that is 31 times larger than prior datasets. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and its strong generalization capability.

Details

Motivation: VLA研究以往主要测试指定任务内容的What级指令，而较少考察规定执行方式的How级指令；作者诊断发现，现有模型对这类指令反应不足，甚至可能降低任务成功率。

Result: 作者假设每个训练片段只配一条指令限制了How级遵循，并通过片段级丰富指令训练，发现动作可对How指令作出响应，但任务成功率没有稳定提升。为超越普通基准衡量此能力，作者提出SIMPLER-IN，要求模型满足仅由指令给出的执行条件。评测显示，获得的响应性不会迁移到更苛刻或分布外的How级指令。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are expected to follow instructions like their language backbones, yet prior work has focused on What-level instructions that specify the task rather than How-level instructions that specify how to carry it out. Through our diagnoses, we find that How-level instructions do not help current VLAs and lower task success. We hypothesize that pairing each training episode with a single instruction limits How-level instruction following, and show that training with segment-level enriched instructions makes actions respond to such instructions without consistently raising task success. To measure How-level instruction following beyond standard benchmarks, we introduce SIMPLER-IN, where success requires conditions given only in the instruction. Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation enables rectified-flow models with diffusion-transformer architectures to combine task-specific capabilities within a single student. However, matching model outputs does not guarantee that their internal representations are aligned, and it remains unclear where and when additional feature supervision is most useful. We analyze the teacher-student feature mismatch across Transformer blocks and denoising timesteps, and find that the mismatch is concentrated in deeper Transformer blocks during early denoising and persists after OPD distillation, indicating ineffective distillation. Motivated by this observation, we introduce FocusOPD, which augments the base OPD objective with token-wise directional feature alignment restricted to deep layers and early denoising steps. The harness equips an LLM agent with tools to query this hierarchy, inspect relevant video, and retrieve clinical records. To evaluate TRACER, we construct two benchmarks. Details

Motivation: 从单张全景图恢复完整360度几何有利于机器人和AR/VR，但经纬度投影图像存在球面畸变；将其切成无畸变透视视图虽可缓解单视图畸变，却常造成视图之间几何不一致。

Abstract: Panoramic 3D reconstruction enables the holistic recovery of complete 360 $^\circ$ scene geometry, offering significant benefits for robotics and AR/VR applications. However, directly estimating geometry from ERP images is inherently challenged by spherical distortions. To reduce spherical projection distortions, a common strategy is to decompose panoramas into distortion-free perspective views, but independently processing these views often introduces cross-view geometric inconsistencies. To address these challenges, we introduce GeoPano, a feed-forward framework for single-view panoramic 3D reconstruction that recovers a geometrically accurate and consistent point cloud from a single panorama. Our approach is driven by two key innovations: First, we propose a panoramic-aware attention aggregator, featuring a streamlined attention architecture equipped with a novel Pano-Attention mechanism, which explicitly exploits the intrinsic spherical geometry of the panorama to guide cross-view interactions, thereby suppressing geometric inconsistencies. Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian representations have made high-quality, real-time volumetric video increasingly practical, yet challenging motion can still break their reconstruction: small fast-moving objects may disappear, and fine textures in highly dynamic regions are often lost. In particular, simply training longer or using more Gaussians may still fail to recover small, fast-moving objects. We argue that the key bottleneck is not representational capacity itself, but where and when Gaussian primitives are allocated. Existing allocation strategies rely on current Gaussian locations and often overlook the spatiotemporal destinations of relocated Gaussians. To address these limitations, we introduce two complementary strategies. First, Seed Injection identifies under-reconstructed dynamic regions using temporal appearance changes and reconstruction errors, and initializes new Gaussians at geometrically plausible 3D locations through multi-view observations, independent of existing Gaussian locations. Second, Temporal Biased Relocation uses time-resolved optimization signals to guide the spatiotemporal placement of relocated Gaussians. Both strategies maintain a fixed Gaussian budget without modifying the underlying representation. Experiments across multiple dynamic-scene datasets demonstrate improved reconstruction quality, particularly in highly dynamic regions, recovering small, rapidly moving objects and fine-grained appearance details.

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models transfer semantic knowledge acquired through vision-language pretraining to robot control, achieving strong performance across various benchmarks. Abstract: Multimodal document retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has focused largely on retrieval, yet its answers depend on how well a vision-language model reads the retrieved pages. We find that evidence in retrieved pages remains underused even when the correct pages are retrieved. On SciEGQA oracle pages, five prior methods give the generator 0.7K to 2.7K visual tokens per query on average under a common image processor, yet raising the page-only budget from 1K to 32K improves accuracy by 15.22 points. At a fixed 16K budget, adding ground-truth crops improves accuracy by 3.75 points over page-only input. We study what the generator needs from retrieved pages through two requirements: Loud, making full use of visual token capacity, and Clear, refining the page so that its evidence becomes prominent. For Loud, increasing either the visual token budget or visual fidelity, the image resolution those tokens preserve, improves accuracy with the other held fixed, and full-page scaling outperforms repetition and tiling at matched budgets. For Clear, suppressing surrounding content improves accuracy without enlarging selected regions, and adding OCR of these regions to page images outperforms either input alone. We implement both as Loud and Clear (LoC), which combines higher-resolution pages with OCR from query-relevant regions without ground-truth annotations or additional training. Details

Result: ZoomCap依据草稿描述检索条件知识、读取OCR敏感文本、补足覆盖空缺，并仅对最复杂的合格区域进行自适应深度裁剪；空间关系从落地几何推导，并融合不重复证据。FactCycle对描述拆出的原子主张分别以源图像（真实性）和重建（可实现性）盲评，将编造与生成器遗漏逐条区分，并辅以人评和多裁判比较。相较Qwen3.5-397B-A17B描述，ZoomCap的教师模型小十倍以上仍取得81.9%人类平局调整偏好分；FactCycle每图计数52.4条传递主张，ScaleCap为31.5。ZoomCap-450K在持续预训练11基准平均分上较最强竞争语料高1.31至2.38分；Ling-Tiny-VL-v3在60B令牌训练后，生产文档/OCR监控套件提升1.4分、感知提升1.5分。医疗预训练中，264万张ZoomCap描述图像使五项VQA基准平均提高3.04分，同图Gemini-3.1-Pro描述提高1.76分。

Abstract: Detailed captions transfer visual information into vision-language model supervision, but quality is easily conflated with length: long single-pass outputs still miss small objects, text, and spatial relations or promote uncertain observations into confident claims. We formulate fine-grained captioning as recoverable visual information transfer, in which a teacher acquires missing evidence under a finite observation budget and converts it into reusable supervision. ZoomCap uses a draft caption to route conditional knowledge retrieval, OCR-sensitive reading, coverage-gap recovery, and adaptive visual zooming, allocating deeper crops only to the most complex eligible region, deriving spatial relations from grounded geometry, and fusing non-duplicate evidence. We also introduce FactCycle, which scores caption-derived atomic claims on two blind anchors–source image (truthfulness) and reconstruction (realizability)–separating fabrication from generator loss claim by claim; human and multi-judge comparisons complement this fact-level view. Against Qwen3.5-397B-A17B captions, ZoomCap achieves an 81.9% human tie-adjusted preference score with a teacher over 10x smaller, and FactCycle counts 52.4 transferred claims per image vs. 31.5 for ScaleCap. Abstract: Although Graph Neural Networks are widely used in various domains, they remained mostly unexplored in computer vision. Convolutional Neural Networks and Vision Transformers were leading the field, until the quite recent introduction of Vision Graphs (ViG) that treat images as a set of nodes and apply graph convolution on their k-NN graphs. ViGs show impressive accuracy on image classification but their computational cost remains relatively high. Inspired by optimization methods for GNNs, we present CoViG, a novel approach combining graph coarsening and ViGs. Abstract: Traditional keypoint descriptors, such as SIFT (Scale-Invariant Feature Transform) and ORB (Oriented FAST and Rotated BRIEF), are computed from local image patches. They may be unreliable in scenes with repetitive patterns or weak textures, where patches at different locations can look similar. We propose the Contextual Line-Assisted Descriptor (CLAD), a keypoint matching method that addresses this problem by using global geometric context from line correspondences. CLAD represents each keypoint with a vector whose elements indicate its proximity to matched lines. It can therefore use the keypoint’s spatial relationships to the lines in the scene. We use HOME (Hough-space One-dimensional Matching of Extrema) to detect both clear and weak linear structures and to establish enough line-to-keypoint associations to represent this context. Evaluations on the TUM RGB-D SLAM dataset and outdoor video sequences show that CLAD provides geometrically consistent matches that complement those obtained with traditional local descriptors. In repetitive scenes, where local descriptors may not distinguish similar patches, CLAD uses global structure to improve matching reliability. The method is computationally lightweight and practical to implement.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has brought strong gains to language model reasoning, but its improvements on visual tasks remain less consistent. One difficulty is that visual task failures can arise from different sources: the same benchmark may be limited by perception for one model and by reasoning for another. We introduce Perception and Reasoning Attribution (PARA), which measures the remaining perceptual and reasoning headroom of each model–benchmark pair by comparing how many current errors are recovered with more accurate visual information versus a stronger reasoner. Across 4 vision-language models and 17 multimodal benchmarks, PARA broadly agrees with the expected characteristics of existing benchmarks while revealing substantial model-specific variation. More importantly, it shows that RL gains become smaller as perceptual headroom increases, but larger when reasoning explains more of the recoverable errors, suggesting that current RL exploits reasoning headroom more effectively than perceptual headroom. Motivated by this gap, we analyze how existing RL objectives affect visual perception and find that reward- and token-level signals influence visual attention only indirectly. We therefore propose GRAD, a group-relative attention distillation objective that uses the best rollout in each group to directly guide the visual attention of weaker rollouts. GRAD consistently improves performance across two model scales and seven benchmarks, and further complements existing RL methods.

TL;DR: 该研究发现类别不平衡微调时，推理阶段换用不同但描述性的提示可恢复稀有类别召回，且其效果来自训练—测试提示交互而非单纯提示质量。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型在类别不平衡数据上微调后，稀有类召回可能低于自身零样本表现；通常推理时沿用微调提示，作者发现这种匹配反而可能比换用另一条描述性提示更差。

Result: 作者在六个数据集及多种视觉语言模型上评估跨提示推理，发现使用不同的描述性推理提示可持续恢复稀有类召回，但对常见类召回存在不同权衡。换成非描述性提示并不会产生相同效果，说明现象不是单纯更换提示带来的。效果在类别不平衡和视觉混淆造成明显稀有类抑制时最强；原本没有此类抑制时则弱或不出现。调优贡献（TuCo）分析显示，跨提示推理减少对微调引入计算的依赖、转向预训练成分；人为降低微调贡献也会显著减小跨提示优势。

Insight: 不平衡微调抑制的稀有类知识可能仍保留在预训练表征中，替换描述提示可改变模型对预训练与微调成分的依赖；稀有类收益需与常见类召回权衡。摘要未报告各数据集的具体增益和类别频率。

Abstract: When a vision-language model is finetuned on class-imbalanced data, rare-class recall can fall well below the model’s own zero-shot performance. In this regime, we uncover a surprising matched-prompt penalty whereby the standard choice of reusing the descriptive finetuning prompt at inference can yield lower rare-class recall than using a different descriptive prompt. We call this inference-time strategy cross-prompt inference. We evaluate diverse VLMs across six datasets and find that cross-prompt inference consistently recovers rare-class recall, with varying trade-offs in frequent-class recall. The effect reflects a train-eval prompt interaction rather than prompt quality alone, and does not arise with non-descriptive prompt changes. It is strongest when imbalance and visual confusability produce substantial rare-class suppression, and is weak or absent when such suppression does not occur. Using Tuning Contribution (TuCo), which decomposes a model’s computation into pretrained and finetuning parts, we find that cross-prompt inference reduces reliance on finetuning-induced computation and shifts the model toward its pretrained component. Reducing that finetuning contribution also substantially reduces the cross-prompt advantage. These results show that rare-class knowledge suppressed by imbalanced finetuning can remain accessible under a different inference prompt.

[68] GRASE: Geometry-native Real-to-sim Agentic Scene Modeling from Video cs.CVPDF
