TL;DR: GRASE以采集视频作为持续参照，形成“渲染—比较—修订”闭环来构建真实到仿真的几何场景模型，逐步校正结构外壳、资产位置与几何误差。

Details

Motivation: 现有真实到仿真转换常采用松耦合的一次性流程，各阶段反馈有限，早期估计误差会逐步传播，最终场景可能与观测不一致。

Result: GRASE将多视角采集视频全程作为场景建模参照，若渲染证据与视频不符，就修订当前假设，而非固定接受中间结果。流程先根据深度及多视角线索拟合建筑外壳和开口；再由智能体在共享坐标系中放置生成资产并检查空间关系；最后迭代细化资产几何和位置以匹配观测视角。各阶段通过反馈闭环连接，定位结构差异并仅修正相关组件，同时维持跨视角一致。作者报告生成场景忠实于输入、视角间一致且可用于仿真，实验显著超过包括GPT-6 Astra直接转换在内的先进方法。

Insight: 持续使用原始多视角视频验证渲染并局部回滚，可减少流水线误差累积，避免早期几何错误被后续阶段固化。摘要未提供场景数量、量化指标、人工成本或仿真任务上的实际收益。

Abstract: Real-to-sim conversion aims to turn a captured video of a real environment into an editable 3D scene while preserving the geometry and layout of what was observed. Existing methods often follow a one-pass, loosely coupled paradigm: each stage receives only limited feedback from previous stages, allowing errors to propagate without reliable correction and leaving the final scene inconsistent. We present GRASE, an agentic framework that closes this loop by treating the captured multi-view video observations as a standing reference throughout scene modeling. Instead of committing to intermediate estimates, GRASE revises its current hypothesis whenever rendered evidence disagrees with the capture, overcoming the fixed intermediate assumptions of conventional pipelines. Specifically, GRASE first fits the architectural shell and its openings from depth and multi-view cues. An agent then places generated assets in a shared coordinate frame while checking their spatial relationships. Finally, each asset’s geometry and placement are refined iteratively to improve agreement across the observed views. By coupling these steps through feedback, GRASE localizes structural discrepancies and revises only the responsible components, while preserving cross-view consistency. The resulting 3D scenes are faithful to the input, consistent across views, and suitable for simulations. In our experiments, GRASE substantially outperforms other state-of-the-art methods, including a direct real-to-sim conversion using GPT6-Astra.

[69] FilterFusion: Prior-Guided Adaptive Filter Fusion for Efficient Video Denoising cs.CVPDF

Wenlong Yang, Weihang Yuan, Canran Jin, Jiahao Li, Henrik Turbell

TL;DR: FilterFusion用低分辨率控制器根据时间/空间方差线索，在原始分辨率自适应混合一组可解释滤波分支，以小模型和规则滤波器实现高效多帧视频降噪。

Details

Motivation: 真实通信视频需同时应对传感器噪声、压缩伪影和运动不一致，并满足严格延迟；纯深度模型精度高但计算大，传统滤波稳健高效却缺乏适应性。

Result: FilterFusion结合时间感知学习特征和可解释降噪滤波分支，预测低成本引导信号，在原分辨率自适应混合少量滤波响应；低分辨率控制器显式使用时间方差和空间方差。模型仅含109K参数。在真实传感器噪声CRVD上，达到34.72 dB PSNR、0.9330 SSIM，网络计算量仅0.18 GFLOPs。滤波器组由高斯模糊、中值及加权求和等简单规则操作构成，直接编译的C合成路径在单CPU线程、1080p下每帧17.9毫秒（55.9 FPS），高于实时要求，速度比每帧超过2秒的轻量CNN基线快122至252倍。

Insight: 低成本控制器负责按场景选择规则滤波响应，可在保留适应性的同时避免全分辨率深网络推理。报告的1080p速度基于单CPU线程及特定编译实现，CNN比较为122—252倍，摘要未给多种混合退化条件下的分项质量。

Abstract: Real-world video denoising for communication systems must handle mixed degradations such as sensor noise, compression artifacts, and motion-induced inconsistency while remaining deployable under tight latency budgets. Purely deep models often achieve high accuracy but come with significant computational cost, whereas classical filters are robust and efficient but insufficiently adaptive. We present FilterFusion, a prior-guided multi-frame denoising framework that combines temporally-aware learned features with an interpretable bank of denoising branches. The model predicts a low-cost guidance signal and uses it to adaptively mix a compact set of denoising responses at the original resolution. The lightweight design uses only 109K parameters and explicitly injects temporal variance and spatial variance cues into the low-resolution controller. On CRVD (real sensor noise), it reaches 34.72 dB PSNR and 0.9330 SSIM with only 0.18 GFLOPs (network-only). Because the filter-bank operations are simple and regular (Gaussian blur, median, weighted sum), a straightforward compiled C implementation of the synthesis path achieves 17.9ms per frame (55.9 FPS) on a single CPU thread at 1080p—exceeding real-time and 122–252 times faster than lightweight CNN baselines that require over 2 seconds per frame. The results demonstrate a strong quality-efficiency-speed trade-off and a practical route to real-time denoising through low-resolution control and explicit filter-based synthesis.

[70] MYOPIA: Understanding the Limits of Tree-Based Reasoning in Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Islam Mesabah, Sary Elmansoury, Gerrit Großmann, Peter Neigel, Sebastian J. Vollmer

TL;DR: MYOPIA基准研究视觉语言模型按人工修订决策树逐问分类的准确率代价，发现树遍历在12种数据集—模型组合中均弱于零样本提示。

Details

Motivation: 决策树可通过根到叶问题序列提供可解释分类，但逐步贪心且不可回溯的过程可能损失准确率，其代价及失败来源尚不明确。

Result: MYOPIA包含针对GTSRB、MNIST、CIFAR-10和MSCOCO构建并经人工修订的四棵决策树，以及匹配提示、诊断协议和评测工具。在GPT-4o、Qwen-VL Max和LLaMA-3.2 11B Vision上，树遍历在全部12种数据集—模型组合中都低于零样本提示，配对bootstrap分析始终支持零样本方法。模型回忆树中类别属性的准确率通常高于将相同属性落地到图像的准确率，平均标签—图像差距分别为9.5、13.8和12.6个百分点。错误局部判断会不可逆地排除正确类别，答案提取也造成额外且依赖模型的损失。节点审计发现，分裂熵与较低节点准确率呈较弱但一致关联，深度、分支因子和节点内grounding差异则较不稳定。

Insight: 层次决策不仅可能因早期错误剪除正确类别，还受视觉属性grounding和答案抽取影响；树结构可解释性不保证分类准确。实验仅覆盖四棵特定树和三类模型，作者建议未来设计优先考虑可感知性及早错恢复。

Abstract: Decision trees can make vision-language model (VLM) classification interpretable by replacing a single prediction with a sequence of root-to-leaf questions. However, the accuracy cost of this greedy, non-backtracking procedure remains unclear. We introduce \textbf{MYOPIA}, a benchmark comprising four LLM-generated, human-refined decision trees for GTSRB, MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MSCOCO, together with matched prompts, diagnostic protocols, and an evaluation harness. Across GPT-4o, Qwen-VL Max, and LLaMA-3.2 11B Vision, tree traversal underperforms zero-shot prompting in all 12 dataset–model pairs; paired bootstrap analyses consistently favor zero-shot. Our diagnostics characterize interacting failure modes associated with this gap. Models often recall tree-encoded class attributes more accurately than they ground the same attributes in images: the mean label–image accuracy gaps are 9.5, 13.8, and 12.6 points for GPT-4o, Qwen-VL Max, and LLaMA, respectively. Incorrect local decisions then irreversibly exclude the correct class, while answer extraction introduces additional model-dependent loss. A node-level audit of the four trees finds that split entropy is weakly but consistently associated with lower node accuracy, whereas depth, branching factor, and within-node grounding differences are less stable. \textbf{MYOPIA} therefore provides a framework for diagnosing hierarchical visual classification and motivates testing tree designs that prioritize perceptual answerability and recovery from early errors. Code is available at https://github.com/islammesabah/VLM_Classification.

Details

Motivation: 现有音视频大语言模型多把音频当作单声道语义内容，限制对声音来源方位及其与视觉场景关系的推理；已有空间音视频研究常只覆盖对应关系或方向距离等单项能力。

Result: 作者提出分层基准HLR-AVSceneQA，评估声音识别、在自我中心和客观坐标视角中的声源定位，以及利用定位结果推理声源与视觉场景的关系。HLR-LLM在强音视频基础模型上加入双耳空间音频分支，同时保留单声道语义音频路径，并以课程学习训练，最后阶段加入多步空间推理思维链监督。实验显示，现有音视频模型在空间落地和关系推理上仍有限；HLR-LLM显著改善空间音视频场景理解，同时不损害语义音视频感知。

Insight: 声音语义识别与声源空间定位是互补能力，需要在模型结构和评测中分别体现；双耳线索及多步推理监督可提升空间理解。摘要未给基准规模、准确率、模型细节及音频空间标注方式。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) have made strong progress in multimodal understanding, yet most treat audio as monaural semantic content, limiting their ability to reason about where sounds originate and how they relate to the visual scene. Recent spatial audio-visual studies address parts of this problem, but often focus on specific abilities, such as spatial correspondence or direction and distance reasoning. In this work, we introduce HLR-AVSceneQA, a comprehensive benchmark with a hear-localize-reason hierarchy that evaluates whether models can recognize what is heard, localize sound sources in egocentric and allocentric views, and use these localized sources to reason about how they relate to the visual scene. We further introduce HLR-LLM, which augments a strong audio-visual foundation model with a binaural spatial audio branch alongside its monaural semantic audio pathway. We train HLR-LLM with a curriculum that incorporates final-stage chain-of-thought supervision for multi-step spatial reasoning. Experiments show that existing AV-LLMs remain limited in spatial grounding and relational reasoning, while HLR-LLM substantially improves spatial audio-visual scene understanding without compromising semantic audio-visual perception.

[72] Spurious Attention Heads in Vision Transformers: Mechanistic Identification of Annotation-Driven Bias in Medical Chest X-Ray Analysis cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Joshua Muhumuza, Cornelius Kalenzi

TL;DR: 机制可解释性分析在ViT-Base/16中定位到专门读取胸片角落标注的注意力头L8H10，并通过遮挡干预验证其因果作用及微调后仍会保留。

Details

Motivation: 医疗视觉Transformer可能利用与疾病无关的伪相关，但若不了解内部机制就难以定位和修复。研究考察模型是否读取胸片角落文字等标注捷径，而非肺部病理。

Result: 在200张COVID-19胸片中，ViT-Base/16的单个注意力头L8H10有62%的情况下关注角落标注而非肺部病灶。修补验证显示驱动因素是标注内容而非亮度或边缘密度；在受影响图像中移除标注使肺部注意力提高1.52倍（p<0.0001）。该现象还见于NIH ChestX-ray14（79%）、DeiT-Base（88%）和ViT-Large/16（80%），跨两个数据集、三种架构重复出现。全量微调未消除此行为，98.4%的伪相关图像仍然存在该现象，伪相关率还略升。单头消融（p=0.340）及三个载体头的多头消融（p=0.532）均无法从输出层面显现该回路。

Insight: 行为准确率评测可能完全看不到具有因果作用的标注读取回路，常规全量微调甚至可能强化捷径；机制审计和内容级干预可揭示隐藏偏差。百分比是作者定义的伪相关图像比例，摘要未给疾病分类性能及不同医院标注分布。

Abstract: Vision Transformers deployed in medical imaging exploit spurious correlations, yet the internal mechanisms remain unidentified and impossible to fix. We apply mechanistic interpretability to locate one such mechanism in ViT-Base 16: a single attention head (L8H10) that functions as a dedicated annotation-reader on chest X-rays, attending to corner text rather than lung pathology in 62% of 200 COVID-19 images. Causal validation via inpainting confirms annotation content rather than luminance or edge density drives the behaviour, increasing lung attention 1.52 times upon removal in affected images ($p{<}0.0001$). The finding replicates across NIH ChestX-ray14 (79%), DeiT-Base (88%), and ViT-Large 16 (80%), establishing spurious annotation-attending heads as a consistent property of ViT-family architectures across two datasets and three architectures. Full fine-tuning does not suppress the behaviour and may entrench it: 98.4% of spurious images remain spurious and the spurious rate rises marginally, as the shortcut is reinforced rather than removed. Critically, single-head ablation produces $p{=}0.340$ and multi-head ablation of all three identified carriers produces $p{=}0.532$, meaning a causally verified annotation-reading circuit is completely invisible to output-level evaluation. Mechanistic auditing finds what behavioural testing cannot.

[73] SCMerge: Spatial-Consistency-Aware Token Pair Merging for Efficient Semantic Segmentation cs.CVPDF

Junfeng Ren, Yi Gong, Yuan Zeng

TL;DR: SCMerge是免训练视觉Transformer令牌合并方法，在配对时同时考虑特征相似性和空间一致性，合并后再映射回原始patch网格用于语义分割。

Details

Motivation: 视觉Transformer的二次注意力复杂度限制语义分割应用；合并相似令牌可缩短序列，但若只依特征相似性而忽略空间一致性，可能引入分割错误。

Result: SCMerge在编码器内部合并成对令牌，配对时共同利用特征相似度与空间一致性，解码后将合并序列重建回原始patch网格。方法无需训练，可插入已有视觉Transformer语义分割流程。在ADE20K、Pascal Context和Cityscapes上的实验显示，与基线相比，SCMerge在分割准确率和效率之间取得更好的权衡。

Insight: 空间相邻或结构一致性应与语义特征共同决定令牌合并，以免将视觉相似但位置关系不同的区域混并。摘要未给令牌缩减比例、延迟/FLOPs及分割分数，效果仅定性报告。

Abstract: Vision Transformer architectures have showcased impressive performance on semantic segmentation, yet their quadratic attention complexity hinders practical applications. Recent works have focused on merging tokens to reduce sequence length and thus improve transformer efficiency. However, existing token merging methods primarily rely on feature similarity to identify similar tokens, overlooking spatial consistency, which may introduce segmentation errors in semantic segmentation. In this work, we propose SCMerge, a training-free token merging method that jointly considers feature similarity and spatial consistency in pairing similar tokens. The pairwise tokens are then merged within the encoder, and the resulting token sequences are reconstructed onto the original patch grid after decoding. The proposed method is flexible and can be plugged into existing vision Transformer-based semantic segmentation pipelines. Extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets ADE20K, Pascal Context, and Cityscapes show that SCMerge consistently achieves a better trade-off between segmentation accuracy and efficiency than baseline methods.

[74] FusionAudit: Pathway-Conditioned Robustness Auditing for Native Multimodal Models cs.CVPDF

Jiaming Zhang, Zherui Li, Siqi Guo, Junhao Dong, Kun Wang

Motivation: 视觉语言模型从冻结编码器流水线转向原生多模态架构后，图像通道既承载视觉内容，也承载可能与用户提示竞争的渲染文字；只用单一像素攻击及单一总分无法覆盖不同攻击面和架构差异。

Result: PaRMA以证据为基础定义路径条件风险，分析像素扰动和渲染文本插入两类图像干预，并使用路径专属诊断暴露架构盲点。研究发现原生模型存在“看见但不受骗”现象：模型强烈关注印刷覆盖文字，却在视觉上拒绝其内容；深度融合模型中编码器级攻击与答案级攻击还存在目标错配。基于此提出边际感知像素扰动MANTIS和宿主一致语义插入Nat-Evid。对18种开放权重VLM及新颖性分类评测显示，传统PGD单一攻击排名在原生模型上不稳定；新攻击显著提高估计的残余风险。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) transition from frozen-encoder pipelines to native multimodal architectures, the visual channel increasingly serves as both a perceptual input and a carrier of rendered text that competes with user prompts. Current single-attack, pixel-centric evaluations fail to capture this complexity: they collapse distinct visual attack surfaces into a single scalar and ignore how architectural shifts alter vulnerability. We introduce PaRMA, an evidence-based auditing framework that defines pathway-conditioned risk over two primary image-level interventions: pixel perturbation and rendered-text insertion. Instead of arbitrary attack enumeration, PaRMA uses pathway-specific diagnostics to expose architecture-specific blind spots. Notably, we uncover a see-but-not-deceived signature where native VLMs attend heavily to typographic overlays but visually reject them, and we identify an objective mismatch between encoder-level and answer-level attacks in deep-fusion models. To exploit these findings, we instantiate FusionAudit, introducing two diagnostic-motivated attacks: MANTIS (margin-aware pixel perturbation) and Nat-Evid (host-congruent semantic insertion). Evaluating 18 open-weight VLMs across a novel nativeness taxonomy, we demonstrate that legacy single-attack rankings (e.g., PGD) are unstable for native models. Our diagnostic-driven attacks substantially raise the estimated residual risk, showing that PaRMA provides a falsifiable, evidence-driven auditing methodology for uncovering vulnerabilities missed by standard benchmarks.

Abstract: We introduce Edit4D, an editable text-to-4D scene generation framework that constructs 4D scenes from separate background and dynamic character representations. Existing approaches typically generate holistic scene videos, where the background and dynamic characters are coupled, making object-level editing and flexible camera control difficult. Edit4D addresses these limitations through a stage-specific generation pipeline. Given a user instruction, Edit4D first decomposes it into stage-specific prompts for background, dynamic character, and scene-character composition. Using these prompts, we synthesize multi-view scene images with text-to-image and camera-conditioned generative priors and reconstruct a background 3D Gaussian splatting representation. We then generate a character image, animate it with an image-conditioned video prior, reconstruct a 4D mesh, and bind Gaussian primitives to obtain a character representation. Finally, both components are composed through text-guided segmentation and contact-aware optimization. A global scene refinement is then performed to improve harmonization and rendering consistency. By integrating stage-specific components, Edit4D enables controllable camera motion, flexible object-level editing, and the practical construction of editable 4D scenes. Abstract: Markerless intraoperative tracking of spinal anatomy via RGB-D sensors is a promising alternative to invasive bone-anchored fiducials, yet no existing clinical dataset provides independent subsurface ground truth for validating these methods. We address this gap with a clinically viable pipeline that derives per-vertebra 6-DoF pose from routine intraoperative C-arm fluoroscopy, co-registered to the RGB-D camera frame through a multimodal fiducial array detectable in both imaging modalities. Critically, the pipeline requires no additional radiation, instrumentation, or deviation from standard surgical workflows, relying solely on the fluoroscopic images already acquired during routine level checks. A calibrated transform chain links the vertebral pose recovered via multi-view 2D-3D CT-to-fluoroscopy registration to the RGB-D coordinate frame, enabling direct comparison of any markerless registration estimate against a subsurface reference. Ex vivo validation against optically tracked ovine vertebrae confirms end-to-end accuracy of 2.06 +- 0.29 mm and 3.35 +- 0.93 degrees, sufficient to serve as independent ground truth for methods with substantially larger errors. We demonstrate the pipeline’s utility by evaluating two markerless registration strategies on clinical data spanning 11 vertebrae across 1,500 frames from three surgical sequences: the first such evaluation against an independent subsurface reference in a clinical setting. The proposed framework provides reusable infrastructure for benchmarking emerging markerless tracking methods on real surgical data.

Abstract: Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence have accelerated video generation technologies. Abstract: As image generation models become increasingly capable of producing highly realistic content, traditional forensic techniques are struggling to keep up. Abstract: Multi-modal semantic segmentation benefits from complementary sensors, but this benefit is fragile when some modalities are degraded or missing at test time. Many existing methods rely on dedicated fusion modules or multi-branch cross-modal encoders, and the fusion often becomes unreliable when modalities are absent, leading to substantial performance drops. We propose \textbf{FuseAdapt}, a parameter-efficient framework that performs fusion within the adaptation space of a frozen vision foundation model (VFM), exploring whether VFM priors can serve as an effective shared backbone for multi-modal segmentation. Instead of attaching a standalone fusion network, FuseAdapt assigns each modality a lightweight adaptation branch and performs \emph{adaptation-space fusion} by keeping Q/V low-rank adaptation private to each modality and routing only FFN low-rank residual activations across modalities inside the frozen backbone. The pretrained VFM remains fixed while modality-specific corrections and explicit cross-modal interaction are learned through the adaptation paths. To handle missing modalities, we further introduce \textbf{Predictive Modality Modeling (PMM)}, a direct representation-level objective for cross-modal redundancy. During training, PMM masks a target modality and predicts its embedding from the visible ones, supervised by a teacher target computed under full-modality input. PMM is used only during training and adds no inference-time overhead. Across three multi-modal segmentation benchmarks (MCubeS, DELIVER, MUSES), FuseAdapt consistently improves both modality-complete and modality-incomplete segmentation with a small trainable budget, yielding up to +3.01 mIoU under full modalities and +12.56 mIoU under missing modalities over prior state-of-the-art. Abstract: We study how vision-language models (VLMs) can learn to think and actively explore in 3D space. Instead of providing multi-view images, videos, or 3D representations to the model, we preserve the native visual encoding of VLMs, i.e., a single image, one at a time, just like humans, and treat the 3D representation only as an environment with which the model can interact through actions (e.g., move left) and observations (rendered images). Each rollout begins from an initial first-person view and a visual question. The model can then iteratively reason, navigate, and reorient its viewpoint before committing to a final answer. Following an initial trajectory-supervised finetuning, we apply reinforcement learning (RL) with rewards driven primarily by final-answer correctness. Crucially, RL provides no direct supervision over which viewpoints the model should visit or which exploration actions it should take. Instead, the model must learn an effective exploration policy solely from the extent to which its interactions improve performance. Across six 3D benchmarks, our model ACT3D achieves an average accuracy of 53.2% that improves its base Qwen model by 22.0% and surpasses proprietary Gemini 3.6 Flash. ACT3D also outperforms existing open-source 3D methods while using 50-70% fewer input frames, and generalizes to unseen domains with an average improvement of 5.9% over its supervised initialization. Further analysis reveals adaptive, question-conditioned exploration. Performance gains over the base model increase from 0.6% on questions answerable from the initial view to 16.3% on those requiring extensive exploration. ACT3D searches for occluded targets, avoids unnecessary exploration, and acquires complementary viewpoints for spatial comparisons. These findings demonstrate that outcome-based RL enables VLMs not only to reason over visual evidence, but also to learn when and how to acquire it.

Insight: 通用视觉表征需要针对部分可见性和形状边界进行适配，低秩自监督调整可改善其匹配用途。摘要未给数据集、指标、具体提升值或左右方向任务定义，SOTA结论范围由所评基准限定。

Abstract: Partial shape matching is a crucial yet underexplored problem in 3D vision, with significant relevance to real-world scenarios where shapes are often only partially observed. Existing feature descriptors face difficulties in this setting, as traditional representations either struggle with the boundaries of partial shapes or heavily depend on the shape’s spatial position. While existing approaches have employed DINO features for partial shape matching, these features are not inherently suited for handling partial observations. In this work, we propose a method to refine DINO features using LoRA-based self-supervised learning, enabling the generation of feature descriptors that are robust to partiality. Our features substantially improve performance on partial shape matching compared to traditional or vision foundation features. Additionally, when integrated into existing partial shape matching pipelines, we achieve state-of-the-art results on partial shape matching and left-right prediction benchmarks.

Abstract: Embodied spatial intelligence requires more than visual recognition: models must localize entities, reason about their relations, and express this knowledge in action-relevant forms. We introduce Anvil, a unified framework for data generation, training, and benchmarking for embodied spatial intelligence. (1) Anvil-Gen is a heterogeneous data flywheel that combines form-specific generation, cross-stage consistency verification, and failure-driven recollection across 11 Ground–Relation–Act forms. (2) Anvil-Ben provides scene-aligned supervision for learning diverse embodied spatial capabilities and scene-linked assessments that distinguish stage competence from error propagation. (3) Anvil-MOPD is a 9B student that consolidates heterogeneous spatial knowledge through distillation from specialist teachers. Semantic-first-fault routing targets teacher supervision to the student’s earliest decisive failure, while stage normalization controls the loss scale across native outputs of different lengths. Abstract: Surgical teams could benefit from assistants that watch the video for requested events, such as “tell me when the clip applier appears during Calot triangle dissection”, and answer questions about what has already happened. It separates procedure-specific perception, which maps the 1-FPS stream to a structured state over tools, actions and phases, from procedure-agnostic reasoning, which evaluates a formal monitoring condition over this state and an event memory, either with exact rules or with a small controller that addresses entities through pointers rather than names. At three external centres, re-training only perception raised performance on held-out procedures from 0.239 to 0.470. Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型的语言先验可能压过视觉证据；既有基准常假设各模型共享先验且只看准确率。作者发现跨模型先验一致率低于80%，且超过15%的题目不看图也能答对，会使准确率高估视觉抗先验能力。

Result: ViSiP-Bench基于六类先验的稀有真实情境，采用模型感知的动态先验诱导协议，并设计三项比准确率更细致、连续的抗先验指标。24个模型评估显示，先验遵从普遍存在，随模型规模增加并未消失；较大模型更倾向于强烈覆盖其先验或坚定遵从先验。作者进一步提出MAPS，先识别每个模型最强先验，再用事实依据充分的知识进行微调，以软化过度先验依赖并尽量不损害通用能力。

Abstract: Language priors in MLLMs can override visual evidence, impairing visual perception. Existing benchmarks assume shared priors across models and rely solely on accuracy. However, cross-model prior agreement falls below 80%, and over 15% of items can be answered correctly without any image, causing accuracy to overestimate prior resistance. To address these limitations, we introduce ViSiP-Bench, a benchmark grounded in rare-but-real scenarios across six prior categories, with a model-aware dynamic prior induction protocol and three metrics that more precisely and continuously measure prior resistance than accuracy alone. Evaluating 24 models reveals that prior adherence is pervasive, does not improve with scale, and larger models tend to either strongly override their priors or firmly adhere to them. We therefore propose MAPS, which identifies each model’s strongest priors and fine-tunes on factually grounded knowledge to soften excessive prior reliance without disrupting general capabilities.

Abstract: Vision–language pre-training has enabled substantial progress in image–text retrieval and zero-shot recognition by learning transferable cross-modal representations. However, sentence-level alignment remains insufficient for compositional matching, where captions can describe the same objects and attributes while differing in how these elements are bound. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-CLIP, which refines global image-text alignment through image-conditioned changes in token–patch evidence. Global Compatibility Calibration (GCC) learns a sentence-level similarity metric, while Image-Conditioned Evidence Refinement (ICER) incorporates visual patch context into token queries and uses the resulting change in local matching evidence to adjust the global score. We further introduce Cross-Image Margin Ranking (CIMR), which compares the same positive–negative caption pair across matched and mismatched images, encouraging a larger ranking margin when the image supports the correct description. Experiments on compositional matching and attribute–object binding benchmarks show that Flow-CLIP improves binding accuracy over pretrained CLIP and learned global alignment through local evidence refinement, while cross-image ranking supervision mitigates reliance on language priors. Abstract: Semantic segmentation is critical for the reliable navigation and scene understanding of autonomous systems, yet its performance degrades severely in low-light environments due to noise, artifacts, colour distortion, and the loss of structural details. To address this challenge, this work introduces VesperMUSE, a unified multi-modal framework designed to achieve robust image semantic segmentation across diverse illumination conditions. The architecture addresses the light deficiency problem on multiple levels: proposing a novel physics-informed synthetic low-light adaptation algorithm, approaching Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) through a thermal-guided paradigm, and finally aiming to recover the intrinsic scene details for semantic segmentation through a multi-modal fusion approach. Following enhancement, the framework dynamically integrates the primary RGB features with auxiliary thermal representations and surface normal maps highlighting geometric discontinuities and material boundaries. At the core of this integration is a highly scalable, modality-agnostic self-attention mechanism that extracts both global context and fine local features from an arbitrary number of auxiliary inputs. By combining and projecting these hierarchical multi-modal representations into a unified component, the attention module allows the network to selectively attend to relevant cross-modal cues without requiring any architectural modifications when different sensor configurations are used. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VesperMUSE successfully recovers missing visual cues, providing a flexible, state-of-the-art (SOTA) solution for machine perception in extreme and unstructured real-world environments.

Abstract: Labeled Sentinel-1 Ground Range Detected (S1GRD) datasets remain scarce, limiting supervised deep learning for land-surface remote sensing, while self-supervised pretraining for this domain has been dominated by Vision Transformer (ViT)-based masked image modeling requiring substantial data and compute. We propose SparK-SAR, a sparse convolutional masked pretraining framework built on SparK and ConvNeXt, designed to exploit convolutional inductive biases for S1GRD imagery under constrained compute. SparK-SAR makes three core technical contributions: (i) ConvNeXt-RS, a domain-adapted encoder that halves the stem stride (4x to 2x) to produce 14x14 feature maps at stride 16x, providing 4x more spatial tokens at identical parameter count; (ii) a lightweight ConvNeXt-512 reconstruction decoder with PixelShuffle upsampling that reduces pretraining VRAM by 7.5-8.5% per GPU relative to a UNet-128 baseline while matching its accuracy; and (iii) an empirical characterization of FP16 mixed-precision pretraining instability in the standard SparK UNet-768 decoder on S1GRD data, complemented by a finding that finetuning with layer-wise learning rate decay (LLRD) improves downstream performance of pretrained encoders. Result: 基准包含100条英文古兰经经文，每条设四种事实状态，并在从纯文本到装饰及权威框架的六种视觉条件下渲染，共2,400张图像；全部引文—出处组合在渲染前经源文本核验和人工审查。24个完整模型—提示运行显示能力分化明显：由API提供的专有VLM从近乎完美核验到中等误接受不等，近期开放权重模型区分能力常较弱，并在多数图像上默认回答VALID。去偏提示可降低误接受，但常是把阈值偏向INVALID，而非形成更强的引用辨别能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret religious, educational, and culturally sensitive visual content, yet their reliability on citation verification remains underexplored. We introduce Visual Sanad, a controlled benchmark for testing whether VLMs falsely accept incorrect Qur’anic verse attributions when the same quote-reference pair is rendered under different visual framings. The benchmark contains 2,400 rendered images built from 100 English Qur’an verses, four factual states per verse, and six visual conditions ranging from plain text to decorative and authority-framed layouts. All quote-reference pairs were source-checked and human-audited before rendering. Across 24 full model-prompt runs, we observe a sharp capability split. Proprietary API-served VLMs range from near-perfect citation verification to moderate false acceptance, whereas recent open-weight VLMs often show weak discrimination and default to VALID on most images. De-biased prompting can reduce false acceptance, but often by shifting the decision threshold toward INVALID rather than by producing strong citation discrimination. Visual Sanad therefore exposes a culturally grounded multimodal factuality failure: fluent VLMs may treat displayed religious citations as self-authenticating unless they have strong citation-verification ability.

Result: 作者在清理后的1,195张图像六分类基准上，以数字标签、视觉线索和Diwani优先三种提示评测12个VLM。数字提示下，闭源/API模型最佳准确率60.9%，开放权重模型最佳47.2%。主要失败是默认标签塌缩：每种数字提示运行都由单一标签主导，占预测33%—63%，而多数类基线为18.8%；12种模型中8种默认选择Thuluth，GPT-4o将52.9%的图像判为Thuluth，尽管数据中其占比仅18.1%。视觉线索提示对部分模型可提升超过10个百分点，对另一些则有害；突出Diwani常只是转移默认标签，并未消除塌缩。

Insight: 总体准确率之外还需检查类别预测分布，以发现系统性默认标签偏置；提示可改善个别模型但不一定提升稳定区分能力。结论限于该六分类、1,195图像基准和三类提示，未评估开放集识别。

Abstract: Arabic calligraphy is a high-stakes cultural visual domain for Muslim communities, yet VLM evaluations rarely test its fine-grained script traditions. We audit 12 VLMs on a cleaned 1,195-image six-way benchmark using numeric, visual-cue, and Diwani-first prompts. In this closed-set setting, the best numeric-prompt accuracy is 60.9% for closed/API models and 47.2% for open-weight models. The main failure is default-label collapse: every numeric run is dominated by a single label taking 33-63% of predictions, against an 18.8% majority-class baseline. For 8 of 12 models the default is Thuluth; GPT-4o labels 52.9% of all images as Thuluth although it is only 18.1% of the set. Prompting helps unevenly: visual cues move accuracy by more than 10 points for some models but hurt others, and foregrounding Diwani often relocates the default rather than removing it. Arabic calligraphy recognition is therefore a compact, culturally grounded stress test for VLM robustness and for measuring which visual traditions become legible to automated systems.

Zhuoming Liu, Jinhong Lin, Lin Zhang, Yiwu Zhong, Yin Li

Motivation: 流式视觉语言模型需要持续整合过去信息并响应当前观察和问题；常见记忆机制将历史压缩、检索或缓存后再放回输入上下文，导致历史记忆与当前观测竞争有限上下文容量。

Result: MaLoW利用循环超网络将新视频观测逐步巩固到固定大小LoRA权重中，历史通过参数更新保留，无需重看完整视频流。为训练构建MaLoW-Instruct，包含56,000段视频上的381,000组问答。在OVO-Bench、StreamingBench和StreamArena上结果较强；OVO-Bench backward划分上，较既有记忆方法跨视频骨干提升3.9%至11.8%，仅有适度延迟开销。方法还迁移至视觉—语言—动作模型，使Simpler WidowX动作成功率提高9.3%。

Abstract: Streaming vision-language models (VLMs) must continuously integrate information from the past while remaining responsive to current observations and user queries. Existing memory mechanisms typically compress, retrieve, or cache historical observations and reintroduce them into the input context, forcing historical memory and current observations to compete for limited context. We propose MaLoW, a streaming memory framework that instead internalizes historical context into evolving lightweight parameter updates. MaLoW uses a recurrent HyperNetwork to incrementally consolidate incoming video observations into fixed-size LoRA weights, allowing historical information to be carried forward without revisiting the full video stream. In doing so, MaLoW represents history as weights, while keeping the current observation as input. To support training, we further construct MaLoW-Instruct, a large-scale streaming video dataset containing 381K question-answer pairs over 56K videos. We conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate that MaLoW achieves strong performance on OVO-Bench, StreamingBench, and StreamArena. On the backward split of OVO-Bench, MaLoW outperforms prior memory approaches by 3.9% to 11.8% across different video backbones while incurring only modest latency overhead. Beyond streaming video understanding, MaLoW also generalizes to vision-language-action models, improving the action success rate on Simpler WidowX by 9.3%.

Abstract: Recent work shows that depth recurrence can be retrofitted into pretrained language models. In this work, we adapt this paradigm for visual understanding, using depth recurrence to reduce the parameter footprint of a pretrained vision-language model (VLM). To this end, we prune layers from the language backbone of the VLM and loop over a subset of the remaining layers. At each step of the loop, a linear adapter merges the block’s output with the token representations from the preceding layers and projects the fused representation back into the block. As the pruning impacts the original model performance, we propose a three-stage recovery training: first, we leverage a language-only pretraining to recover the performance of the language backbone, followed by an alignment of the vision-language projector, and a full fine-tuning of the model via visual instruction tuning. In the first and the last stage, we distill the outputs of the pretrained non-recurrent model into the recurrent model, which further improves the recovery. We evaluate the proposed approach on the TinyLLaVA-1.4B backbone without introducing any new vision-language data during retrofitting. Our experiments demonstrate that the retrofitted recurrent model outperforms the unpruned baseline trained with the same recovery data while storing 23.3% fewer language parameters. We further show that the proposed retrofitting recurrence outperforms the setup of converting a recurrent LM into a VLM on the same data. Additionally, our ablations show how design choices, such as the adapter between recurrences and a larger recurrent block, lead to the improved performance of our approach. Finally, we find that our proposed model gains on reasoning-oriented benchmarks, whereas its performance on basic visual understanding benchmarks remains on par with the baseline.

Abstract: Whole-slide images may contain coexisting lesions, yet weak supervision typically provides only the most severe diagnosis. We formulate this setting as dominant-label multiple instance learning, where the observed label is the maximum-severity element of a latent lesion set. Standard multiclass MIL treats it as a complete one-hot description and therefore suppresses lower-severity evidence whenever it co-occurs with a dominant lesion. We propose DomSet-MIL, a label-aware framework that aligns the annotation mechanism across bag and instance levels. Dominant-Label Censored Likelihood models the reported diagnosis without declaring unreported lesions absent. Severity-Ordered Residual Routing assigns patch evidence from high to low severity and preserves residual support for secondary findings, while top-$q$ pooling links local routing scores to bag prediction. Abstract: Contrastive vision-language pre-training supports zero-shot chest X-ray diagnosis, but standard objectives treat every off-diagonal image-report pair in a batch as a negative. In radiology, recurring findings across patients mean that instance mismatch need not imply semantic incompatibility, creating false-negative supervision that we term a semantic conflict. A training-trace audit shows that pair-observations selected by the online mask used during training are enriched for report-derived finding agreement, although this proxy analysis does not establish clinical equivalence. Fixed thresholds can become miscalibrated as similarity distributions evolve, while soft reweighting leaves residual repulsive gradients. We therefore propose Semantic-Conflict-Aware Contrastive Learning (SCCL), which tracks modality-specific similarity distributions with exponential-moving-average (EMA) statistics, derives adaptive thresholds, and hard-masks flagged candidates before softmax normalization. Inclusive (OR) and consensus (AND) rules define two SCCL teachers, whose bidirectional cross-modal rankings are consolidated through standard distillation into a single deployable student, SCCL-DT. Across five public chest X-ray benchmarks, SCCL-DT achieves a mean zero-shot AUC of $0.850$, compared with $0.837$ for the strongest evaluated baseline in our comparison. Code will be made publicly available soon.

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation often relies on an encoder’s final global representation, leaving complementary information in intermediate layers underused. Combining these features is challenging because their dimensions can differ and their usefulness varies across tasks. We propose LAMP, Layerwise Adaptive Mixing of Predictors, a training-free framework that combines global and regional information from multiple encoder layers. A single forward pass per view supplies all descriptors. Global and support-calibrated regional kernel predictors independently fit the same label residuals. Labeled validation data select their mixture in class-score space, preserving native feature dimensions without learned projections. LAMP-F further adapts text-anchored class prototypes, using a cached response to update the fixed-kernel residual predictions exactly. Experiments cover eleven datasets and ViT and ResNet backbones. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy among the compared published training-free ResNet-50 methods at 4, 8, and 16 shots, reaching 78.73% at 16 shots. Averaged across five support budgets, LAMP improves over a paired global-feature-only baseline by 1.14 and 1.59 percentage points on ViT-B/16 and ResNet-50; LAMP-F raises these gains to 1.96 and 2.54 points. LAMP-F reaches 16-shot accuracies of 83.57% and 79.10%, respectively. Controlled experiments identify multi-layer integration as the main source of improvement and examine pooling, calibration, validation labels, and computational cost. These findings show that combining layer-specific predictions can exploit complementary features from frozen encoders to improve few-shot classification.

Motivation: 细长结构对连通性错误高度敏感，少量局部错误也可切断原本连续路径；传统骨架损失固定监督中心线位置，没有考虑可恢复连通性的替代局部路径。

Result: TRACE根据目标骨架定义必需局部连接，对每条连接在当前预测中寻找最容易成立的有效重连路线，并联合监督该路线瓶颈及相应骨架点，使监督随预测变化。作者还提出拓扑感知骨架载体，保留前景和类别特定拓扑，以可靠定义连接。在六个二维及三维基准上，实验显示该目标改善连通性和拓扑，同时保持较强分割准确率，训练额外开销较低。

Insight: 自适应重连监督不把模型固定拉向单条中心线，而可利用当前预测中更容易恢复的路径来修复连通。摘要未给基准名称、拓扑/准确率指标及训练成本的具体数值。

Abstract: Thin, elongated structures are particularly sensitive to connectivity errors, as even a few local segmentation mistakes can disrupt otherwise continuous paths. Existing skeleton-based objectives typically supervise fixed centerline locations and do not account for alternative local routes that may restore connectivity. We propose TRACE, a topology-aware, prediction-adaptive objective that uses a target skeleton to define required local connections. For each connection, TRACE identifies the easiest valid reconnection under the current prediction and jointly supervises its bottleneck with the corresponding skeleton point. The supervision thus adapts to the evolving prediction rather than remaining fixed to the centerline. We further introduce a topology-aware skeleton carrier that preserves both foreground and class-specific topology, enabling reliable definition of these connections. Extensive experiments across six 2D and 3D benchmarks show that TRACE improves connectivity and topology while maintaining strong segmentation accuracy with low training overhead. Code will be released upon acceptance.

Abstract: Accurate motion prediction is critical for safe autonomous driving, yet even strong forecasters leave residual errors in their multimodal proposals. Existing refiners compress complete proposals or update their segments sequentially, preventing the full proposed future from serving as explicit relational context for correction. We introduce FutureLift, a backbone-agnostic refiner that represents each multimodal proposal as a heterogeneous future graph, with spatially grounded temporal nodes connected to agent histories, map elements, alternative modes, and, for joint prediction, other agents future trajectories. Abstract: Physical understanding is crucial for decision making, yet vision models struggle to infer properties such as damping that govern how materials behave. Audio encodes such properties directly, because an object’s acoustic response to impact reveals its damping through measurable decay. [108] Routing Policy Optimization for Online Diffusion Reinforcement Learning cs.CVPDF

Qiuhong Shen, Yiding Yang, Yuxin Zhang, Xinwei Huang, Minxuan Lin

TL;DR: RPO把扩散生成器内部的注意力路由作为随机探索动作，在少数去噪层采样注意块并以成对rollout奖励差更新策略，而推理时恢复原确定性稠密注意力。

Details

Motivation: 用于视觉生成对齐的策略梯度方法通常扰动反向去噪轨迹来引入随机性，需要高成本rollout优化；作者探问能否在生成器内部探索，同时保持原采样器。

Result: Routing Policy Optimization在选定去噪层中让每个查询块保留自身块，并按模型原生注意力质量采样其它键块，或选择稠密注意力。采样路由改变该转移速度，再用围绕稠密速度的高斯似然为策略梯度打分。受控双rollout估计器让两个轨迹共享提示、初始潜变量、路由层和稠密锚定层，仅注意路由采样不同，奖励差作为路由转移优势。因似然对稠密速度本身求导，移除稀疏路由后学习更新仍有效；推理时恢复稠密注意力及原确定性采样器。文生图和文生视频实验显示质量提升，训练效率较Flow-GRPO类方法近4倍。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can align visual generators with reward signals beyond their pretraining objectives, but existing score-function methods typically introduce stochasticity by perturbing the reverse denoising trajectory, leading to costly rollout-based optimization. We instead ask whether stochastic exploration can be introduced inside the generator while preserving its original sampler. Routing Policy Optimization (RPO) answers this question by turning attention routing into a stochastic exploration action. At a few selected denoising levels, each query block keeps its own block and samples further key blocks in proportion to the generator’s native attention mass, or keeps dense attention. The sampled route perturbs the velocity of that transition, and a Gaussian likelihood around the dense velocity scores the perturbation with a score-function objective. RPO further uses a controlled two-rollout estimator: two rollouts share the same prompt, initial latent, routed denoising levels, and dense anchor layers, differing only in their sampled attention routes. Their reward difference provides an advantage signal for the routed transitions. Because the likelihood differentiates the dense velocity itself, the learned updates remain effective after routing is removed. At inference, RPO restores dense attention and retains the original deterministic sampler. Experiments on text-to-image and text-to-video generation show that RPO improves generation quality while achieving nearly 4x higher training efficiency than Flow-GRPO-style methods. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/rpo for visual results.

[109] Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition in Videos cs.CVPDF

Jay N. Paranjape, Jurijs Nazarovs, Rohan Sarkar, Yue Guo, Lipeng Ke

TL;DR: GHADAR定义按人定位后对视频中每个人描述特征及活动的任务，构建基于AVA-Actions的开放集数据集，并在训练中用定位约束视觉语言模型的交叉注意力掩码。

Details

Motivation: 既有视频数据多只支持群体活动识别、单人活动识别或封闭集分类，无法要求模型对复杂场景中的每个人分别生成有定位依据的特征和活动描述。

Result: GHADAR任务要求给出每个人在视频中的位置，并描述视频里所有人的特征和活动。作者基于AVA-Actions生成开放集人物及活动描述数据，并提出训练方法，在VLM训练期间约束交叉注意力掩码以利用定位信息。实验显示其超过先进VLM。作者还指出现有评测指标过度依赖人工注释和文本精确匹配，因此提出直接在视频与生成描述间比较概念的整体VLM评估方案。

Insight: 人物级定位可为开放式描述提供实体依据，训练中显式利用grounding也有助于每人属性和活动的归属；评估需关注视频概念匹配而不只是文本重合。摘要未给数据集规模、基线分数或VLM评估器的人类一致性。

Abstract: Understanding characteristics and activities of individuals in complex multi-person environments is crucial for real-world applications. However, existing datasets often simplify this problem to grounded group activity recognition, single-person activity recognition, or close-set activity recognition, thus limiting the generalizability of models trained on these datasets. In this work, we introduce the task of Grounded Human-Attributed Description and Activity Recognition (GHADAR), which involves describing the characteristics and activity descriptions of \textbf{every} person in a video, provided the location of the person in the video, which is more practical for real-world applications. To facilitate this, we introduce a new dataset derived from AVA-Actions by generating open-set captions for the person description and activity. In addition, we propose a novel method to effectively utilize the information contained in grounding during training by constraining the cross-attention masks during training in VLMs to improve performance for this task. Our experiments show that our method outperforms SOTA VLMs on this task. Finally, we demonstrate the limitations of existing evaluation metrics, which are overly reliant on human-annotations and exact text-text matching. As an added video-based evaluation, we propose a holistic VLM-based evaluation schema that compares concepts {\em directly} between the video and the generated predictions. Thus, in this work, we develop a complete framework for GHADAR, including a dataset, a novel method and an evaluation schema, thereby establishing a strong foundation for future research in this domain.

[110] Velox: Spatial Memory Makes World Models Efficient cs.CVPDF

Qiuhong Shen, Shizun Wang, Xinchao Wang

TL;DR: Velox利用流式几何的视锥深度片段对应关系，路由长视频生成中与当前区域相关的历史注意力块，避免KV缓存随rollout增长导致后续片段变慢。

Details

Motivation: 交互世界模型需生成响应迅速且视觉连贯的长视频；分块视频扩散模型通常保留不断增长的KV缓存，导致越往后生成越慢。

Result: 免训练注意力路由器Velox把历史块表示为含深度区间的相机视锥片段，并通过近期优先多重warp估计当前块深度。对历史覆盖良好的区域，视锥重叠软调制模型推导的块重要性；未覆盖区域则保持原模型路由、不加几何偏置。固定令牌块粒度与FlashAttention式分块执行匹配，减少随rollout长度增长的注意力计算。视锥构建与去噪在同一H200上异步运行。到第48块时，注意力加速4.36倍、去噪加速2.43倍；WBench导航全158例上，平均表现距离稠密LingBot-Fast-Cam不超过0.15个免API分数点。

Insight: 几何可用于选择历史记忆块，使模型重点读取与当前可见区域相关的缓存内容，并以异步处理降低路由开销。第48块加速是特定长rollout位置，摘要未给其它长度、质量分项及显存变化。

Abstract: Video world models turn actions and camera motion into long visual rollouts, making video a natural interface for interactive simulation. Such interaction requires responsive generation, yet coherent rollouts must retain visual memory of what has already been generated. Chunked video diffusion models usually keep this context in growing KV caches, so later chunks become progressively slower. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Velox, a training-free attention router for region-level context selection using frustum slab correspondences from streaming geometry. Velox represents historical blocks as camera frustum slabs with depth intervals and obtains current-block depth through recency-priority multiwarp. For well-covered regions, frustum slab overlap softly modulates each layer’s model-derived block importance. Uncovered regions retain the model-derived route without geometric bias. The fixed token-block granularity matches FlashAttention-style tiled execution and reduces the attention term that grows with rollout length. Frustum slab construction runs asynchronously with denoising on the same H200. At chunk 48, Velox accelerates attention by $4.36\times$ and denoising by $2.43\times$. On the full 158-case WBench navigation split, it remains within 0.15 API-free average points of dense LingBot-Fast-Cam. Please see our project page at https://velox-world.com/velox/ for more details.

[111] Not Just a Subject: Capturing Adaptive Scene-Specific Variance for Visual Storytelling cs.CVPDF

SeungJu Cha, Ye-Chan Kim, Kwanyoung Lee, Dong-Jin Kim

TL;DR: EOS-Story在文字和视觉两个空间分离人物身份与场景变化：将短语外观残差注入场景EOS嵌入，并按场景尺度适配身份特征，减少视觉叙事中的结构伪影。

Details

Motivation: 扩散模型可生成视觉故事图像序列，但既有方法偏重人物身份保持，难适应不同场景变化，导致语义错配和结构灵活性不足。

Result: 免训练EOS-Story先将叙事拆成身份和场景子提示，避免场景内容被身份信息稀释；为让场景也能获得外观线索，Cross-Contextual Bridging将短语级外观残差软注入场景EOS嵌入。视觉空间中，作者发现身份主体与场景主体尺度不匹配会反复转移少量身份令牌，形成结构伪影；Adaptive Identity Preservation通过场景尺度身份锚定及身份感知自注意力整合特征。作者扩展Consistory+基准，加入多样构图场景Consistory++。实验显示EOS-Story在两个基准上均达到有竞争力或更优表现。

Insight: 人物一致性应随场景尺度适配，而非机械复制身份令牌；文本子提示和视觉特征整合分别解决语义稀释与尺度错配。摘要未给具体指标、基线和训练/推理成本，且称方法免训练。

Abstract: Recent advancements in diffusion models have significantly improved text-to-image generation, enabling their application to visual storytelling, where coherent image sequences are generated from narrative prompts. However, existing methods primarily focus on identity preservation and often fail to adapt to diverse scene variations, resulting in semantic misalignment and structural inflexibility. We propose EOS-Story, a training-free framework that harmonizes identity consistency with scene-specific adaptation across text and vision spaces. In the text space, we decouple the narrative into identity and scene sub-prompts to prevent information dilution, but this leaves the scene without access to appearance cues. To bridge this gap, we introduce Cross-Contextual Bridging, which softly injects a phrase-level appearance residual into the scene’s embedding. In the vision space, we find that scale mismatch between identity and scene subjects causes a few identity tokens to be repeatedly transferred, producing structural artifacts. We address this with Adaptive Identity Preservation, which anchors identity features at the scene-specific scale (Identity Anchoring) and integrates them via Identity-Aware Self-Attention. To evaluate performance under varied spatial compositions, we extend the Consistory+ benchmark with diverse framing scenarios (Consistory+_+). Extensive experiments show that EOS-Story achieves competitive or superior performance on both benchmarks.

[112] AVC-Reward: Event-Centric Audio-Video Consistency Evaluation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yuruo Wu, Kaihang Pan, Kai Shen, Jiacheng Li, Shuyuan Tu

TL;DR: AVC-Reward以事件为单位拆解视听生成内容，分别检查整体叙事协调、事件时间同步和细粒度属性对齐，并将评价训练成可用于在线RL的奖励模型。

Details

Motivation: 联合音视频生成可呈现多事件、多层音频和不对称跨模态关系，但当前评估多沿用简单任务指标，整段打分且预设少量关系，难以评价复杂场景。

Result: 作者提出事件中心框架，分别分解音频与视觉事件、逐事件分析并跨模态交叉核验关系，最终聚合为粗到细的三级一致性指标，评估全局叙事协调、事件级时间同步和细粒度属性对齐。基于多类生成模型产生的正负样本构建AVC-Benchmark，并将框架实例化为可训练AVC-Reward。结果显示其自动评价与人类判断的一致性优于其它自动方法，能够给出定位失败原因的可解释依据；作为在线强化学习信号时，也提升联合音视频生成模型的一致性。

Insight: 复杂音视频评估需检查每个事件及事件之间的跨模态关系，整体平均分可能掩盖局部错位；可解释奖励还能用于优化生成器。摘要未报告基准规模、人类一致性数值及RL前后具体指标。

Abstract: As joint audio-video generation models become more capable, they can generate audio-visual scenes exhibiting three forms of complexity: multi-event composition, multi-layer audio mixture, and asymmetric cross-modal relations. However, current evaluations largely reuse metrics from earlier simple and unidirectional tasks. They typically evaluate an audio-video clip as a whole and rely on simple predefined relations, making them unsuitable for these increasingly complex scenes. We propose an event-centric evaluation framework that disentangles audio and visual events at the event level, analyzes each event independently, cross-validates relations across modalities, and aggregates the judgments into a coarse-to-fine, three-level consistency metric. The metric assesses global narrative harmony, event-level temporal synchronization, and fine-grained attribute alignment, providing a general formulation for audio-video consistency evaluation across diverse generation settings and cross-modal relations. We instantiate the metric as AVC-Reward, a trainable audio-video reward model. To evaluate the agreement between automatic judgments and human perception across generation paradigms and relation complexities, we construct AVC-Benchmark, which is primarily built from diverse positive and negative samples generated by various types of audio-video generation models. AVC-Reward achieves state-of-the-art alignment with human judgment among automatic methods, provides interpretable rationales that localize failures, and improves the audio-video consistency of a joint generation model when used as an online reinforcement-learning signal. Project page: https://tsuki0512.github.io/AVC-Reward.github.io/

[113] Dense4D: Pixel-Level 4D Human Correspondence cs.CVPDF

Rıza Alp Güler

TL;DR: Dense4D从多张单目RGB图像预测人体、衣物和头发的稠密表面点图及深度，以共同参考时刻的三维表面坐标建立跨姿态、形变和遮挡的对应关系。

Details

Motivation: 4D人体重建需在关节运动、身体变形及可见性变化中追踪同一表面点；既有方法通常依赖身体模板，难以统一处理身体、衣物和头发。

Result: Dense4D是专为人体稠密4D对应设计的前馈模型，不需要人体模板。模型联合处理经过人物中心裁剪、并已知原图到裁剪变换的多张图像，逐帧预测Human Point Map（HPM）及深度；HPM为人物像素赋予共同参考时刻下其观测表面的3D位置。即使参考帧看不到某点，也可从其它帧定位对应位置；跨裁剪匹配HPM坐标即可建立对应，不必预测相机姿态或大范围场景位移，之后用逐图深度将匹配提升为3D轨迹。真实人物实验中，作者报告其跟踪准确度明显高于基线，且更接近扫描表面；不同帧点可组成参考姿态下连贯人体几何。

Insight: 通过共同参考时刻的表面坐标对齐各帧，使对应关系不依赖相机姿态估计，并可利用其它帧恢复参考视图中被遮挡的点。摘要未提供数据集、跟踪/表面误差数值和与基线的定量差异。

Abstract: Reconstructing human motion in 4D requires identifying the same surface points through articulation, deformation and changes in visibility. We introduce Dense4D, to our knowledge the first feed-forward model dedicated to dense 4D correspondence across the body, clothing and hair from a set of monocular RGB images, without a body template. Trained specifically for human geometry, Dense4D jointly processes person-centred crops with known image-to-crop transforms to predict Human Point Maps (HPMs) and depth for each image. An HPM assigns each person pixel the 3D position of its observed surface point at a common reference time. These maps support amodal correspondence: points hidden in the reference image can be localised from observations in other frames. Matching HPM coordinates across crops establishes correspondence without predicting camera poses or large scene displacements; per-image depth then lifts the matches into 3D tracks. Experiments on real people demonstrate substantially higher tracking accuracy and closer agreement with scanned surfaces than the evaluated baselines. Points from different frames assemble into coherent human geometry in the reference pose, providing visible evidence of consistent dense correspondence through articulation and clothing deformation. Additional qualitative results, videos, and an interactive viewer are available at https://alpguler.com/dense4d.

[114] ACEdit: Stable Multi-Turn Image Editing via Asymmetric Conditioning cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.GRPDF

Sara Dorfman, Nir Goren, Gal Barak, Daniel Cohen-Or, Or Patashnik

TL;DR: ACEdit通过训练期让学生在自身上一轮编辑结果上继续工作，并让教师始终以原始干净图像和组合指令提供监督，缓解多轮图像编辑中的分布偏移。

Details

Motivation: 图像编辑模型单轮表现良好，但每轮会引入小的分布变化并逐步累积，使多轮推理输入偏离原始训练分布，质量和指令遵循随轮次退化。

Result: 方法采用Self-Forcing思路，训练编辑器处理自己之前生成的结果。困难在于自生成编辑轨迹会偏离真实轨迹，直接监督不可靠；作者因此引入教师编辑器蒸馏信号。非对称条件设计中，学生逐步编辑自己的输出，教师则始终锚定原始干净图像并接收复合编辑指令，提供相对稳定目标。实验显示，该训练方案显著降低长多轮序列中的质量退化，同时保持编辑质量和指令遵循。

Insight: 学生与教师不必使用相同的多轮输入分布：学生需适应自身输出，教师可保留原图作为稳定参照。摘要未说明编辑轮数、数据集、具体质量指标及教师调用/训练成本。

Abstract: Image editing models produce impressive results for individual edits, but their quality often deteriorates over multiple editing turns. Each editing turn introduces small distribution shifts that accumulate over successive edits, creating a gap between the input distributions encountered during the editor’s original training and multi-turn inference. We introduce a training scheme that makes multi-turn image editing stable under this accumulating distribution shift. A key is to force the editor to operate on its own outputs from previous editing turns during training, bringing the principle of Self-Forcing to image editing. The main challenge is that self-generated editing trajectories deviate from ground-truth trajectories, making direct supervision unreliable. We address this challenge through a distillation objective that leverages a teacher image editor to provide a reliable training signal when the model operates on its own previous outputs. We introduce an asymmetric conditioning scheme: while the student performs edits sequentially on its own outputs, the teacher remains anchored to the original clean image and receives a compound editing instruction. Experiments show substantially reduced degradation over long multi-turn sequences while maintaining editing quality and instruction adherence.

[115] 3DCodeVerse: Exploring 3D Worlds as Code cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yipeng Gao, Ziyao Zeng, Jinfa Huang, Yijiang Li, Haolin Xiong

TL;DR: 3DCodeVerse提供大规模可执行3D程序数据、带自动验证的多智能体编码harness和经验指南，支持智能体以代码形式生成可编辑、可检查的三维内容。

Details

Motivation: 让编码智能体创建3D内容需要高质量可执行程序、空间推理工具和自动验证流程，单靠自然语言描述或静态3D数据难以规模化探索。

Result: 3DCodeVerse-2M整理独立可执行3D代码，来源包括开源数据、专用表示转换及编码智能体生成；每个程序均附执行环境所需源文件和资产，以及可用的描述和验证记录。3DCodeVerse Harness是多智能体有向无环图流程，利用执行检查、空间测量和视觉反馈自动评价及修订程序，生成过程无需人工反馈。系统还进行3D编码实证研究并建立经微调开源模型基线。作者报告，在Claude Opus 5.5驱动两种方案的成对人评中，3DCodeVerse harness相较Claude Design（3D Object）获90%胜率。

Insight: 可执行程序、配套资产和运行验证记录能把3D生成转成可复现的代码任务；自动执行与几何/视觉反馈有助于迭代纠错。90%是由同一Claude Opus 5.5使用两套系统时的成对人评结果，摘要未给数据集筛选、评审人数及开源模型定量结果。

Abstract: We introduce 3DCodeVerse, a research ecosystem that enables coding agents to create editable and verifiable 3D content at scale. 3DCodeVerse comprises large-scale, high-quality datasets of executable 3D code, a coding harness for spatial reasoning and automated verification, and practical guidance for 3D coding. First, we construct 3DCodeVerse-2M, a large-scale curated dataset of standalone 3D executable code. We build this corpus by collecting open-source data, converting specialized representations, and generating new programs with coding agents. Each program is packaged with the source files and associated assets required for execution in its declared environment, along with available descriptions and verification records. Second, we develop the 3DCodeVerse Harness, a multi-agent directed acyclic graph framework for generating and refining 3D content. It uses execution checks, spatial measurements, and visual feedback to automatically evaluate and revise generated programs without human feedback during generation. Third, we conduct systematic empirical studies of 3D coding, establishing baselines with fine-tuned open models and deriving practical guidance through component-level analyses of our harness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning on our dataset significantly improves the 3D coding capabilities of open-source models. Notably, our 3DCodeVerse harness achieves a 90% win rate against Claude Design (3D Object) in pairwise human evaluations, when both are utilized by Claude Opus 5.5.

[116] Speculative Forcing for Efficient Autoregressive Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Hyunwook Choi, Seungryong Kim, Dongyoon Han, Sangdoo Yun

TL;DR: Speculative Forcing把中间去噪的干净估计视为视频生成草稿，预测哪些令牌可提前冻结并将其缓存继续作为上下文，以减少逐令牌不必要的完整去噪计算。

Details

Motivation: 少步自回归视频生成可流式输出，但常对每个令牌都运行到最终去噪步；作者发现不少令牌在初始步骤已接近最终输出，而另一些才需要继续细化。

Result: Speculative Forcing允许已接受令牌作为最终结果冻结、不再继续去噪，同时将其缓存的键值保留为其它令牌上下文。作者提出两种验证器：免训练方案根据干净内容相似度及跨步稳定性判断；学习式方案从冻结生成器中间特征估计令牌距离最终输出的剩余差距。短视频、长视频和世界生成实验中，方法在显著降低计算时保持全步采样质量。LongLive上以每令牌约1.7步去噪匹配四步生成器，速度超过1.5倍，达到41.7和37.8 FPS；Lingbot-World 2.0在持续变化的视角内容下以每令牌约2.5步保持质量，加速1.35倍、达到24.5 FPS。

Insight: 提前接受已稳定令牌并保留其KV作为上下文，可按令牌分配去噪计算，而非全体等量迭代。FPS及步数对应指定模型/设置；摘要未给验证器误接受率及不同内容区域的收益。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generators enable streaming generation, yet they run every token through the final denoising step, regardless of how much refinement it needs. We find this uniform computation is often unnecessary: many tokens already closely match their eventual full-step outputs in the initial steps, while others require refinement until the end. We introduce Speculative Forcing, a framework that treats intermediate clean estimates as drafts and predicts when individual tokens can be safely accepted early. Once accepted, a token is frozen as its final output and undergoes no further denoising, while its cached keys and values remain available as context for the remaining tokens. We explore two alternative verifier designs: a training-free one based on similarity to clean content and stability across steps, and a learned one that reads the frozen generator’s intermediate features to estimate each token’s remaining gap to its full-step output. Across short-video, long-video, and world generation, Speculative Forcing preserves the quality of full-step sampling while substantially reducing computation. On LongLive, it matches the four-step generator with ~1.7 denoising steps per token, running over 1.5x faster at 41.7 and 37.8 fps. On Lingbot-World 2.0, where camera motion continually changes the visible content, it maintains quality with ~2.5 denoising steps per token and runs 1.35x faster at 24.5 fps.

[117] SUPRA-TEX: 3D Generation with Super-Aligned Texture cs.CV | cs.GRPDF

Chen Yang, Jiemin Fang, Fanpeng Meng, Zihao Huang, Jichen Pang

TL;DR: SUPRA-Tex把单图3D材质重建拆为可见材质估计、表面投影对齐及基于观测的3D补全，以固定可见证据锚点生成完整可重光照材质。

Details

Motivation: 单图3D生成需为给定网格赋予可重光照材质并忠实保留参考细节；多视图预测不一致会在融合时产生伪影，而隐式图像特征条件又难保留细节。

Result: 作者先训练多模态大模型图像编辑器，在参考视角预测去除光照影响的PBR材质图，再投影到可见表面，并通过预训练材质VAE编码成与生成目标同一潜空间的观测潜变量。交错流Transformer将这些潜变量作为只读固定锚点，在未观测区域更新与全表面更新间交替，以生成完整材质潜变量。方法旨在对可见区域实现贴合参考图的所见即所得保真，同时对不可见表面作连贯补全。仅用公开数据训练时，作者报告其在几何条件纹理生成和端到端资产生成上均达SOTA，并在野外图像上匹配或超过领先商业系统。

Insight: 把可见材质作为同空间只读锚点，可将生成模型容量集中于保留观测及补全不可见区域，避免多视图不一致融合。摘要没有数据集、指标和商业系统对比细节，SOTA及野外结果无法定量展开。

Abstract: Single image 3D generation aims to dress a given 3D mesh with relightable materials while faithfully preserving the visual details of a reference image. Existing methods either introduce artifacts when fusing inconsistent multi-view predictions or struggle to preserve fine details through implicit image-feature conditioning. To resolve these challenges, we introduce SUPRA-Tex, built on the principle of decoupling visible-material estimation, surface alignment, and observation-guided 3D completion. This division of labor allows the 3D generator, trained on limited 3D data, to focus its capacity on preserving visible evidence and completing unobserved surfaces. Specifically, we first train an MLLM-based image editing model to predict lighting-separated PBR maps in the reference view. We then project these maps onto the visible surface and encode them with the pretrained material VAE encoder into native observation latents that share the generation target’s latent space. An interleaved flow transformer holds these latents as fixed, read-only anchors while alternating unobserved-region and full-surface updates to generate complete material latents. This design achieves super-alignment with the reference, delivering What You See Is What You Get fidelity on visible regions and coherent completion on unobserved surfaces. Trained solely on public datasets, SUPRA-Tex achieves SOTA performance in both geometry-conditioned texturing and end-to-end asset generation, matching or even exceeding leading commercial systems on in-the-wild images. Project page: https://supra-3d.github.io/supratex/

Abstract: Vision-language models still struggle to understand 3D spatial relations across views and update spatial judgments as the observer moves. A common approach fuses 3D reconstruction features into visual tokens, but aligning these features with pretrained semantic representations remains challenging under limited spatial supervision. We propose DK-VLM with Dual-Kernel Attention, which computes attention from both a visual token’s input position and its geometric position in the scene. A semantic kernel retains the pretrained model’s native attention and positional encoding, while a separate geometric kernel uses estimated 3D points and camera poses to directly modulate attention through point displacements and relative view transformations. Then, a learned gate fuses their outputs. Geometry thus enters attention as position rather than as fused reconstruction features. We further introduce Reference-Frame Composition (RC) supervision to train the model to update its reference frame across successive judgments. It provides stepwise turn and heading targets derived from verified single-step annotations. Among the compared methods, DK-VLM-8B achieves the highest average score across six spatial benchmarks and the highest overall scores on VSI-Bench (74.33) and ReVSI-32 (58.28). Such feedback must integrate multi-view observations and distinguish genuine inconsistencies from projection-dependent appearances. We make three contributions. First, we introduce 3DVerifierBench, a benchmark spanning 10 tasks that evaluates decisions, rejection explanations, and responses to controlled changes in specifications and candidates. [121] SoftCache: Adaptive Step Size Caching for Diffusion Transformers cs.CVPDF

Shimon Malnick, Nadav Orenstein, Ohad Fried, Shai Avidan

TL;DR: SoftCache利用缓存陈旧度信号距阈值的余量控制自适应采样步长：余量大时跨越多个计划节点，接近刷新时缩小步长，而不是仅二元决定缓存或重算。

Details

Motivation: 免训练缓存能重用近期网络激活以加速扩散/流模型，但动态缓存通常把陈旧度信号只作为刷新阈值的开关，且每一步仍按固定预设步长推进。

Result: 作者观察到陈旧度信号包含更多信息：其距刷新阈值的余量可预测复用产生的误差。SoftCache据此将余量映射为缓存步长；余量大时跳过多个计划节点，接近刷新则逐渐恢复为原步长，允许采样轨迹偏离固定网格。方法无需训练，也不需按骨干调参。在FLUX图像生成及HunyuanVideo、Wan 2.1视频生成中，3.5至6.9倍加速范围内，其四项保真指标均领先所有比较基线。

Insight: 缓存复用程度可以连续调节，而不是只用固定阈值作“刷新/不刷新”判断；误差余量可进一步用于跳步采样。摘要未给每个模型、速度点的质量数值及步长控制实现细节，3.5—6.9倍是多设置范围。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow models generate a sample over iterative steps, each a full pass through a large network, and that pass dominates generation cost. Training-free caching accelerates them by reusing activations from recent evaluations instead of recomputing them. Dynamic caching methods decide when to refresh by accumulating a cheap probe of the network input into a staleness signal and spending a fresh evaluation once it crosses a threshold. They perform hard caching: the signal is a binary switch between refresh and reuse, and every step, fresh or cached, advances by the prescribed step size. We observe that the signal carries much more than this switch. How far it sits below the threshold, its headroom, predicts the error induced by reuse. We propose SoftCache, which performs soft caching: instead of a binary switch, the headroom sets how far each cached step goes. Where the headroom is large, the sampler jumps past several scheduled nodes, and as a refresh nears, its steps shrink back toward the original step. These adaptive jumps may land between the scheduled nodes, taking the sampler off its fixed grid. SoftCache requires no training and adds no per-backbone settings: on FLUX for images and on HunyuanVideo and Wan2.1 for video, at speedups from $3.5\times$ to $6.9\times$, it is ahead of every baseline on all four fidelity metrics.

Abstract: In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.

[124] When Reasoning Traces Conflict with Evidence: How Answer Configuration Shapes Evidence Following cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Shaoang Li, Yanhang Shi, Jian Li

TL;DR: 该研究把推理轨迹的两个作用分开测量：它可能提高答案准确率，也可能在与新证据冲突时妨碍模型更新；不同答题配置对证据跟随能力影响显著。

Details

Motivation: 保留的推理轨迹既可作为有用线索，也可能在答案阶段压过新证据；仅删除轨迹或看最终准确率，无法判断答案究竟跟随轨迹还是当前证据。

Result: 作者交叉设置经核验的不同证据版本和模型先前生成的推理轨迹，在相同轨迹及证据下比较继续原思考与单独新答案调用。三个模型家族gpt-oss、Gemma4、Qwen3在HotpotQA和MuSiQue上，单独答案调用的证据跟随率比继续思考高29.9至60.6个百分点。移除推理轨迹在两种配置下都会以相近幅度降低准确率；但冲突时，继续思考多数重复过时结论，而单独调用更常采用当前证据。进一步分析发现，轨迹末行保留其大部分价值，黄金证据若在轨迹写出前提供最有帮助；干扰证据会削弱单独调用的纠错能力；重新推理在多数冲突中会采用当前证据，而单纯要求验证则不会。

Insight: 使用推理轨迹获益与在证据冲突时服从轨迹是不同属性，评测应同时报告二者及答案调用方式。删除轨迹会丢失有用信息，不代表它没有产生锚定效应；结果限于两个QA基准和指定模型。

Abstract: A reasoning trace retained at answer time plays two roles: it can raise accuracy, and it can compete with the current evidence when the two disagree. We disentangle these roles by crossing verified evidence versions with the traces generated from each and answering from the same traces and evidence under two configurations: continuation of the model’s own thinking and a separate answer call. Across three model families (gpt-oss, Gemma4, Qwen3) on HotpotQA and MuSiQue, the separate call raises evidence-following over continuation by 29.9 to 60.6 points. On the same pairs, removing the trace lowers accuracy by similar amounts under both configurations. Yet continuation repeats the stale conclusion in most conflicts, and the separate call follows the current evidence far more often. Benefiting from a trace and deferring to it are therefore distinct properties. Further analyses show that the final line of a trace carries most of its value, gold evidence helps most before the trace is written, distractors weaken correction under a separate call, and renewed reasoning, unlike a verification instruction, follows the current evidence in most conflicts. Because trace deletion alone cannot reveal which source an answer follows, evaluations should report both properties together with the answer configuration.

Abstract: Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

[126] SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: SkillNEO将智能体技能优化设为非精英式演化，分别选择实际使用的版本和继续编辑的版本，从相似版本在同一任务上的表现中提取应保留步骤与待修问题。

Details

Motivation: 技能改进常需多个相互依赖编辑，低奖励版本可能仍是有价值的中间状态；若必须每次编辑立即带来收益，就会过早丢弃尚未完成的修复路径。

Result: SkillNEO把“选择使用哪个技能”与“选择继续改进哪些技能”分开，比较相关版本在同任务上的表现，找出可保留步骤及残余失败，并可依据后续证据回访早期版本。86项、覆盖8领域的SkillsBench任务上，GPT-5.4 Mini平均奖励从49.1%升至66.4%，达到GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6 Luna原始技能的范围。41项修改技能任务上，复用改进使Luna提升10.5个百分点，GPT-5.5较其发布的原始技能参考高10.6点。相同任务实例用于最终评估。搜索记录显示，最终获选技能有时经由分数较低的中间版本；技能也能在更强模型上复用，即使Mini仍未解决部分要求。

Insight: 技能搜索应保留低分但可能含有局部有效改动的版本，不能只做即时奖励贪心选择；较弱模型的部分进展可转成更强模型可复用程序知识。最终评测使用相同任务实例，摘要未说明相对于搜索过程可能产生的数据/任务泄漏控制。

Abstract: Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini’s average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna’s original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model’s partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.

Insight: 该结果展示的是受控104小时活动中智能体团队进行数据、代码及实验工程的自主能力，并非发现全新算法的证据；系统服务、去污染和隐藏评测对结论至关重要。比较的分数来自所述六任务开发与留出套件，摘要未给各任务表现和额外算力成本。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement, in which AI systems build stronger AI systems, is one promising route to superintelligence. We study a weaker form that can be measured today: frontier agents post-training a small raw base model with public data, as a test of their research potential. We build Athanor, an open agent-native system for autonomous post-training research (AutoPostTrain) on real GPUs. We give the agents full research autonomy, low-friction engineering tools, and efficient starter code. The agents carry out the research themselves: they read datasets and evaluation results, build their training data, write and modify training algorithms, tune hyperparameters, and analyze the results to decide what to try next. They work in a sandbox and cannot use external teacher models. Athanor’s services supply frozen evaluation, managed jobs, decontaminated data, a persistent research record, and a held-out suite the agents are unaware of. In a 104-hour campaign, a team of four Claude Opus 5.5 agents, each on an eight-GPU node and given no research advice, improves Qwen3-4B-Base from 15.9 to 67.3 on a six-task development suite and from 9.2 to 48.2 on a held-out suite, against 66.5 and 47.9 for the official Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration that a team of frontier agents can autonomously post-train a raw base model into a cross-domain generalist model that matches or surpasses the official instruct version on average. The campaign’s full record shows that the agents reach this level by recombining known methods and each other’s models: SFT on public reasoning data, RL with a rollout change one agent introduced, weight merges across agents, and rounds of self-distillation. With execution handled, today’s agents already have the engineering and research ability to conquer this task. We hope Athanor provides a foundation for scaling autonomous AI research.

Abstract: Agent memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experiences without updating their parameters. However, semantic similarity alone does not guarantee memory usefulness: downstream performance depends on how memories are selected and presented to the agent. We propose MemComposer, a memory composer that jointly examines the current query and retrieved candidates to decide which experiences to include, how many to select, and how to order them. We train the composer using Experience Policy Optimization (ExPO), a GRPO-based framework that evaluates assembled memory contexts through complete agent interactions and uses task-success feedback to optimize the assembly policy. Across three $τ^2$-bench domains with two frozen executors, MemComposer outperforms the strongest baseline for each executor while reducing average memory-token usage by 16.9% and 4.9%, respectively. Motivation: 模型允许用户决定思考多长时间，但额外推理如何影响日常语言含意尚不清楚；例如“有些学生通过”通常暗示并非全部通过，逻辑字面含义与会话语用推断可能不同。

Abstract: When a speaker says ``some students passed’’, listeners infer that not all of them did. Reasoning models now let users decide how long a model thinks before it answers, but we do not know what extra thinking does to such everyday inferences. We study this directly: we keep a model’s weights fixed, change only how much it thinks, and record which reading it chooses. A second tool cuts the model’s own reasoning at successive points and asks for an answer, showing where the reading changes. Thinking makes models read logical words as logic: it cancels the implicatures of \emph{some} (\emph{not all}) and \emph{or} (\emph{not both}) in most tested open reasoning models and in GPT-5, while context-driven inferences such as indirect answers improve. The trace shows why: models first entertain the enriched reading and cancel it later. Answering from that early reading, an exploratory inference-time remedy, recovers these implicatures without losing literal accuracy. Evaluations of reasoning models should measure how they interpret, not only how accurate they are.
